Sharp estimates for screened Vlasov-Poisson system around Penrose-stable equilibria in ℝ d \mathbb{R}^{d} , d ≥ 3 d\geq 3
Lingjia Huang, Quoc-Hung Nguyen and Yiran Xu

Abstract
In this paper, we study the asymptotic stability of Penrose-stable equilibria among solutions of the screened Vlasov-Poisson system in ℝ d \mathbb{R}^{d} with d ≥ 3 d\geq 3 that was first established by Bedrossian, Masmoudi, and Mouhot in [ 8 ] with smooth initial data. More precisely, we prove the sharp decay estimates for the density of the perturbed system, exactly like the free transport with only Hölder (i.e., C a C^{a} for 0 < a < 1 0<a<1 ) perturbed initial data. This improves the recent works in [ 21 ] by Han-Kwan, Nguyen, and Rousset for lower derivatives of the density and in [ 15 ] by T. Nguyen for higher derivatives with a logarithmic correction in time. Furthermore, we establish new estimates and cancellations of the kernel to the linearized problem to obtain this result. Moreover, we also prove this result for the Vlasov-Poisson system in which the electric field obeys a general nonlinear Poisson equation containing massless electrons/ions case.



1
Introduction

This paper is devoted to the study of the asymptotic stability of equilibria for the Vlasov-Poisson system of the form:

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}f_{i}+v\cdot\nabla_{x}f_{i}+E\cdot\nabla_{v}f_{i}=0\\
E=-\nabla_{x}U,-\Delta U+U=\rho_{i}-1+A(U),~~\rho_{i}(t,x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}f_{i}(t,x,v)dv\\
\end{cases}

(1.1)

on the whole space
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,
v\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
,
d\geq 3
, where
f_{i}=f_{i}(t,x,v)\geq 0
is the probability distribution of charged particles in plasma,
\rho_{i}(t,x)
is the electric charge density, and
E=E(t,x)
is electric field and
A:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}
is smooth and satisfies
A(r)=\circ(r)
as
r\to 0
. In particular, massless electrons/ ions case if
A(r)=r+1-e^{r}
; screened case if
A(r)=0
; and unscreened case if
A(r)=r
.

This system describes a hot, unconfined, electrostatic plasma, ions in
d
dimensions of electrons on a uniform, static, background of ions with
d\geq 3
. This system has been extensively studied (
[
1
,
4
,
23
,
2
,
10
,
19
,
3
,
38
,
39
,
24
,
14
,
32
,
20
,
17
,
18
,
28
,
13
]
), focusing on the global existence, regularity results and longtime behavior of solutions.
The first paper on the global existence of weak solutions with
A(r)=r
is due to Arsenév in
[
1
]
. Then Batt in
[
4
]
established global existence for spherically symmetric data, which was extended by Horst in
[
23
]
to global classical solvability with cylindrically symmetric data. After that in
[
2
]
, Bardos and Degond obtained global existence for small data with
d\geq 3
by using the Lagrangian approach. Next, in
[
38
]
Pfaffelmoser proved the global existence of a smooth solution with
large (unrestricted size) data. Later, simpler proofs of the same were
published by Schaeffer
[
39
]
, Horst
[
24
]
, and Lions and Pertharne
[
32
]
. Moreover, the system with massless electrons ( i.e
A(r)=r+1-e^{r}
) in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
with
d\leq 3
was also studied by Bouchut in
[
10
]
, (see also
[
20
,
17
]
).
In recent years, in
[
26
]
they indicated the sharp faster decay of derivatives. Later on, in
[
12
]
they extended the results of (
1.1
) to
d\geq 2
with better electric field decay in the case of screened interactions. Furthermore, the vector fields approach in
[
40
]
and the Fourier analysis approach in
[
41
,
28
]
were also developed in the last few years.

In this paper, we study the stability and the long time behavior of solutions
f_{i}
to (
1.1
) in the form

f_{i}(t,x,v)=\mu(v)+f(t,x,v),

where
\mu(v)
is a stable equilibrium with
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mu(v)dv=1
and
f_{i}(t=0,x,v)
closes to
\mu(v)
. So,
f
solves the following perturbed system

\begin{cases}\partial_{t}f+v\cdot\nabla_{x}f+E\cdot\nabla_{v}\mu=-E\cdot\nabla_{v}f,\\
E=-\nabla_{x}U,-\Delta U+U=\rho+A(U),~~\rho(t,x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}f(t,x,v)dv,\\
f|_{t=0}=f_{0}.\end{cases}

(1.2)

Here are our assumptions in this paper.

Assumption 1

\mu
satisfies the Penrose stability condition:

\inf_{\tau\in\mathbb{R},\xi\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left|1-\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-i\tau s}\frac{1}{1+|\xi|^{2}}i\xi\cdot\widehat{\nabla_{v}\mu}(s\xi)ds\right|\geq\text{\r{c}},

(1.3)

for some constant
\text{\r{c}}>0
,
\widehat{\nabla_{v}\mu}
is the Fourier transform of
\nabla_{v}\mu
in
\mathbb{R}^{d}
.

Assumption 2

\mu\in L^{1}
satisfies

\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{N}\nabla_{v}\mu(\cdot)\|_{W^{2,\infty}}+\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{d+5}\nabla\mu(\cdot)\|_{W^{2d+7,1}}+\frac{1}{N-d}\leq M^{*},

for some
N>d
and
M^{*}<\infty
.

Assumption 3

A:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}
is
C^{2}
and satisfies

\displaystyle\sup_{|r|\leq 1}\left(\left|\frac{A(r)}{r^{2}}\right|+\left|\frac{A^{\prime}(r)}{r}\right|+\left|{A^{\prime\prime}(r)}\right|\right)\leq C_{A},

for some constant
C_{A}>0
.

Note that a particular example for the assumption
3
is
A(r)=r+1-e^{r}
corresponding to the Vlasov–Poisson system with electrons mass, see
[
10
]
.

It is well-known that the free transport equation
\partial_{t}f+v\nabla_{x}f=0
exhibits phase mixing which the spatial density
\rho(t,x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}f(t,x,v)dv
decays in time. It was an observation of Landau in
[
31
]
that the linearized Vlasov-Poisson equations near homogeneous Penrose stable equilibria also have the spatial density decaying in time. Under the Penrose condition (
1.3
), nonlinear Landau damping was proved on
\mathbb{T}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}
in the pioneering work
[
33
]
by Mouhot and Villani for data with Gevrey regularity (see also
[
9
,
15
]
for refinements and simplifications). In
[
6
]
Bedrossian showed that the results therein do not hold in finite regularity (see also
[
16
]
)). We note that related mechanisms in the fluid are the vorticity mixing by shear flows
[
5
,
27
,
30
,
34
]
. In the whole space
\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}
with
d\geq 3
, it was established for the screened Vlasov-Poisson system in
[
8
]
by Bedrossian, Masmoudi, and Mouhot for data with finite Sobolev regularity. Their proof relies on the dispersive mechanism in Fourier space to control the plasma echo
resonance. However, a decay in time of their result is far from optimal, as the dispersion in the physical space was not taken into account. In the recent work
[
21
]
, Han-Kwan, Nguyen, and Rousset revisited the asymptotic stability of Penrose stable equilibria. They obtained the decay estimates for the density
\rho
as follows:

\|\rho(t)\|_{L^{1}}+(1+t)\|\nabla\rho(t)\|_{L^{1}}+(1+t)^{d}\|\rho(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}+(1+t)^{d+1}\|\nabla\rho(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\lesssim\varepsilon_{0}\log(t+2),~~\forall~t>0.

(1.4)

This is achieved by a pointwise dispersive estimate directly on the resolvent kernel for the linearized system around Penrose stable equilibria
\mu
. Moreover, (
1.4
) is optimal up to a logarithmic correction. Very recently, in
[
29
]
Ionescu, Pausader, Wang, and Widmayer proved the first asymptotic stability result for the unscreened Vlasov-Poisson system in
\mathbb{R}^{3}
around the Poisson equilibrium, see also in
[
37
]
for the case of a repulsive point charge. The unscreened case is open for the general equilibria. However, in
[
22
,
7
]
they studied the linearized unscreened Vlasov-Poisson equation around suitably stable homogeneous equilibria in
\mathbb{R}^{d}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}_{v}
.

Our goal in this paper is to prove (
1.4
) for (
1.2
)
without

\log(t+2)
(in RHS). We recall the Besov norm and Triebel-Lizorkin norm as

\|g\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{s}}:=\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}g(x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}}{|\alpha|^{s}},\|g\|_{\dot{F}_{p,\infty}^{s}}:=\big\|\sup_{\alpha}\frac{|\delta_{\alpha}g(x)|}{|\alpha|^{s}}\big\|_{L^{p}_{x}},

and for
\kappa\in(0,1)
we define a norm weighted in time:

\|g\|_{\kappa}=\sum_{p=1,\infty}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\left(\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|g(s)\|_{L^{p}}+\langle s\rangle^{\kappa+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|g(s)\|_{\dot{B}^{\kappa}_{p,\infty}}\right),~~~\|g\|_{l,\kappa}=\sum_{j=0}^{l}\|\langle s\rangle^{j}\nabla^{j}g\|_{\kappa}.

By Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequality, we know that

\|g\|_{l,\kappa}\sim\|g\|_{\kappa}+\|\langle s\rangle^{l}\nabla^{l}g\|_{\kappa}\sim\sum_{p=1,\infty}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\left(\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|g(s)\|_{L^{p}}+\langle s\rangle^{l+\kappa+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|\nabla^{l}g(s)\|_{\dot{B}^{\kappa}_{p,\infty}}\right).

Let us introduce some notations.

Notation 1.1

Let
g:\mathbb{R}^{d}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}_{v}\to\mathbb{R}^{m}
. We define, for
(x,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{d}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}_{v}
and
a\in(0,1)

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{a}_{1}g(x,v)=\sup_{z\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{|g(x,v)-g(x-z,v)|}{|z|^{a}},~~\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{a}_{2}g(x,v)=\sup_{z\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{|g(x,v)-g(x,v-z)|}{|z|^{a}},

\displaystyle\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{a}g=\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{a}_{1}g+\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{a}_{2}g,\mathcal{D}^{a}g=|g|+\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{a}g,\mathcal{D}^{a}_{i}g=|g|+\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{a}_{i}g,~~i=1,2.

Our main result is as follows.

Theorem 1.2

Let
a\in(\frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2},1)
. There exist
C_{0}>0
,
\widetilde{\varepsilon}\in(0,1)
such that for
0<\varepsilon\leq\widetilde{\varepsilon}
if

\sum_{p=1,\infty}\sum_{i=0,1}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{a}(\nabla_{x,v}^{i}f_{0})\right\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}}\leq\frac{1}{C_{0}}\varepsilon,

(1.5)

then the problem (
1.2
) has a unique global solution
f
with

\|\rho\|_{a}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|U\|_{a}\leq\varepsilon~~\text{and}~~\|\rho\|_{1,a}+\|U\|_{1,a}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},a,M^{*}}1.

In addition, with
\tilde{\varrho}_{0}(t,x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}f_{0}(x-tv,v)dv
, we have the estimate that

\displaystyle\|\rho-\tilde{\varrho}_{0}-G*_{(t,x)}\tilde{\varrho}_{0}\|_{a}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|U\|_{a}\lesssim\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}},

(1.6)

where the kernel
G
is defined in (
2.6
).
Moreover, for
m\in\mathbb{N}^{+}
and
b\in(0,1)
if

\begin{split}&\sum_{j=2}^{m+1}\sup_{|r|\leq 1}\left|{A^{(j)}(r)}\right|\leq C_{A,m},~~\quad\sum_{j=0}^{m}\sum_{p=1,\infty}\|\mathcal{D}^{a}(\nabla_{x,v}^{j}f_{0})\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}}\leq\mathbf{c},\\
&\quad\|\langle v\rangle^{d+\gamma+5}\nabla\mu(\cdot)\|_{W^{2d+2\gamma+7,1}}+\sum_{j=1}^{m+3}\sup_{v\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\langle v\rangle^{N}|\nabla^{j}\mu(v)|\leq\mathbf{c}^{\prime},\end{split}

for some
N>d
, then

\|\rho\|_{m,b}+\|U\|_{m,b}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},a,M^{*},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime}}1.

Remark 1.3

If we have initial data
f_{0}
with higher-order derivatives, we can get more regularity of the solution
\rho
. It is different from the condition in
[
36
]
, which made smallness assumptions on higher derivatives of
f_{0}
.

Remark 1.4

The crucial decay estimates of
E(t,x)
depend on the fact that we work in dimension
d\geq 3
. And the decay will be stronger when
d
becomes higher. The decay estimates are insufficient in the case
d\leq 2
which can be seen in the proof of Proposition
4.1
, where we need to estimate
\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a-\delta}}
. That is the reason why we only consider
d\geq 3
in our paper. We deal with the 2d case in
[
25
]
.

Remark 1.5

While finishing our article, we learned that Toan Nguyen was working on this problem and had a similar result to ours. But the two works are independent.

As
[
21
, Corollary 1.1]
, thanks to Theorem
1.2
, we also obtain the following scattering property for the solution to (
1.2
). The proof is omitted as it is very analogous to
[
21
, Proof of Corollary 1.1]
.

Corollary 1.6

With the same assumptions and notations as in Theorem
1.2
, there is a function
f_{\infty}\in W^{1,\infty}
given by

f_{\infty}(x,v)=f_{0}(x+Y_{\infty}(x,v),v+W_{\infty}(x,v))+\mu(v+W_{\infty}(x,v))-\mu(v),

such that

\|f(t,x+tv,v)-f_{\infty}(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\frac{\varepsilon}{\langle t\rangle^{d+a-1}},~~t\geq 0,

and
\|Y_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|W_{\infty}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\varepsilon
.

Let us discuss the main idea in this paper. First, we give the equivalence of
(\rho,U)
and
(f,\rho)
satisfying the system

\displaystyle(\rho,U)=\left(\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U),\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho,U)\right),

\displaystyle~\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U)=G*_{(t,x)}((\mathcal{I}+\mathcal{R})(\rho,U))+(\mathcal{I}+\mathcal{R})(\rho,U),

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho,U)=(1-\Delta)^{-1}(\rho+A(U)),

where the kernel
G
is defined in (
2.6
) and
\mathcal{I},\mathcal{R}
are defined in (
2.3
). This means,
(\rho,U)
is a fixed point of
\mathcal{F}=(\mathcal{F}_{1},\mathcal{F}_{2})
. We establish the boundedness of the mapping
\mathcal{G}(\cdot)=G_{(t,x)}*\cdot
under the norm
\|\cdot\|_{a}
. To do this, we need to estimate some pointwise decay estimates of the kernel
G(t,x)
, which gives a new proof to be different from the scaling method introduced by Han-Kwan, Nguyen, and Rousset in
[
21
]
. It should be emphasized that the cancellation of
G(t,x)
, i.e.
\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}G(t,x)dx=0,~~\forall t\geq 0,
and the estimate of
\||x|^{a}|\delta_{\alpha}G(t,x)|\|_{L^{p}_{x}}
play a very important role in proving the boundedness of the mapping
\mathcal{G}
. Using the decay estimates for the characteristics to prove maps
(\rho,U)\mapsto\mathcal{I}(\rho,U),~(\rho,U)\mapsto\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)
be compressed mappings with a small contracting constant.
Finally, we apply the contracting mapping principle to get a fixed point of
\mathcal{F}
.

The paper is organized as follows. In section 2, we establish the equivalence of
(\rho,U)
and
f
, then we focus on the estimate of
(\rho,U)
in the following sections. In section 3, we give the pointwise decay estimates of the kernel
G(t,x)
and its derivatives. Then, we prove the bound:

\|\mathcal{G}(\mathbf{f})\|_{\gamma,a}\leq M\|\mathbf{f}\|_{\gamma,a},

for some constant
M=M(\text{\r{c}},d,M_{1})
. In section 4,
we establish the pointwise estimate of the characteristics
\left(X_{s,t}(x,v),V_{s,t}(x,v)\right)
and their derivatives. In section 5, we estimate the contribution of the initial data and explain that why we need the norm
L^{1}_{x}L^{1}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{1}_{x}\cap L^{1}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{\infty}_{x}
of the initial data
f_{0}
.
In Section 6, we estimate the reaction term
\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)
and its higher derivatives. To handle it, we shall introduce a general map

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t,x)=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F(s,X_{s,t}(x,v))\eta(V_{s,t}(x,v))dvds+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F(s,x-(t-s)v)\eta(v)dvds,

and give the estimate in Proposition
6.1
. In Section 7, we prove the main theorem.

The following are some notations in our paper.

Notation 1.7

Throughout this paper,
A\lesssim B
means that there exists constant
C
only depending dimension
d
such that
A\leq CB
.
A\lesssim_{M}B
means that there exists constant
C=C(d,M)
such that
A\leq CB
. The same convention is adopted for
A\gtrsim B
and
A\sim B
.

Notation 1.8

In this paper,
\widetilde{\cdot}
is the ”space-time” Fourier transform on
\mathbb{R}^{d+1}
as follows

\displaystyle\widetilde{g}(\tau,\xi)=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}e^{-i\tau t}e^{-ix\cdot\xi}g(t,x)dxdt.

And we define inverse Fourier transform

\displaystyle\mathscr{F}^{-1}(h)(t,x)=\frac{1}{(2\pi)^{d+1}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}e^{i\tau t}e^{ix\cdot\xi}h(\tau,\xi)d\xi d\tau.

Notation 1.9

For multi-index
\alpha=(\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2},...,\alpha_{d})
, we denote
~|\alpha|=\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+...+\alpha_{d}.

Notation 1.10

For a vector
x\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
, and
k\in\mathbb{N}
, we define

x^{\otimes k}=\underbrace{x\otimes x...\otimes x}_{k}
.

: Data will be made available on reasonable request.

2
Equivlance of
(\rho,U)
and
f

In this section, we will show that the system (
1.2
) is equivalent to an equation in terms of
(\rho,U)
. Let
\left(X_{s,t}(x,v),V_{s,t}(x,v)\right)
be the flow associated to the vector field
(v,E(t,x))
, i.e.
\left(X_{s,t}(x,v),V_{s,t}(x,v)\right)
satisfies the ODE system:

\begin{cases}\frac{d}{ds}X_{s,t}(x,v)=V_{s,t}(x,v),\qquad&X_{t,t}(x,v)=x,\\
\frac{d}{ds}V_{s,t}(x,v)=E(s,X_{s,t}(x,v)),\qquad&V_{t,t}(x,v)=v,\end{cases}

(2.1)

for any
0\leq s\leq t
,
(x,v)\in\mathbb{R}^{d}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}
.
Then, the solution
f
of the equation (
1.2
) is given by

f(t,x,v)=f_{0}(X_{0,t}(x,v),V_{0,t}(x,v))-\int_{0}^{t}E(s,X_{s,t}(x,v))\cdot\nabla_{v}\mu(V_{s,t}(x,v))ds,

(2.2)

(see (1.3) in
[
35
]
with
\mathbf{B}(t,x,v)=(v,E(t,x))
).
We denote

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\mathcal{I}(\rho,U)(t,x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}f_{0}(X_{0,t}(x,v),V_{0,t}(x,v))dv,\\
&\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)(t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(E(s,x-(t-s)v)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mu(v)-E(s,X_{s,t}(x,v))\cdot\nabla_{v}\mu(V_{s,t}(x,v))\right)dvds.\end{split}

(2.3)

Hence,
\rho(t,x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}f(t,x,v)dv
is the solution of the following equation:

\begin{split}\rho(t,x)&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}[\nabla_{x}(1-\Delta_{x})^{-1}(\rho+A\circ U)](s,x-(t-s)v)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mu(v)dvds+(\mathcal{I}+\mathcal{R})(\rho,U)(t,x)\\
&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}[\nabla_{x}(1-\Delta_{x})^{-1}(\rho+A\circ U)](s,w)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mu(\frac{x-w}{t-s})\frac{1}{(t-s)^{d}}dwds+(\mathcal{I}+\mathcal{R})(\rho,U)(t,x)\\
&=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}(\rho(s,w)+A\circ U(s,w))\left(1-\frac{1}{(t-s)^{2}}\Delta_{v}\right)^{-1}\Delta_{v}\mu(\frac{x-w}{t-s})\frac{1}{(t-s)^{d+1}}dwds\\
&\quad+(\mathcal{I}+\mathcal{R})(\rho,U)(t,x).\end{split}

(2.4)

Define
K(t,x)=\frac{1}{t^{d+1}}\left(\left(1-\frac{1}{t^{2}}\Delta_{v}\right)^{-1}\Delta_{v}\mu\right)\left(\frac{x}{t}\right)\mathbf{1}_{t>0}
, then
\rho
can be expressed in the following way

\rho=\big(K*_{(t,x)}(\rho+A(U))\big)+\mathcal{I}(\rho,U)+\mathcal{R}(\rho,U).

Next, taking Fourier transform in
(t,x)
, we have

\displaystyle\widetilde{\rho}=\widetilde{K}\widetilde{\rho}+\widetilde{K}\widetilde{A(U)}+\widetilde{\mathcal{I}(\rho,U)}+\widetilde{\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)}.

Then we have

\rho=G*_{(t,x)}(\mathcal{I}(\rho,U)+\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)+A(U))+\mathcal{I}(\rho,U)+\mathcal{R}(\rho,U),

(2.5)

where
G
is the kernel satisfying:

\widetilde{G}=\frac{\widetilde{K}}{1-\widetilde{K}},

(2.6)

and

\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)=\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-i\tau t}\frac{1}{1+|\xi|^{2}}i\xi\cdot\widehat{\nabla_{v}\mu}(t\xi)dt.

Thanks to the Penrose condition, one has

|1-\widetilde{K}|\geq\text{\r{c}}>0,
which implies that

\widetilde{G}
is well defined.

It is clear that if we have
f
, we can obtain the formula of
\rho
and
U
. On the other hand, if we have
\rho
and
U
, we can get
E
, then we recover the characteristics
\left(X_{s,t}(x,v),V_{s,t}(x,v)\right)
to finally get
f
from (
2.2
).

Remark 2.1

We will use
\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{N}\nabla_{v}\mu(\cdot)\|_{W^{2,\infty}}
to estimate the reaction term
\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)
, and

\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{d+5}\nabla\mu(\cdot)\|_{W^{2d+7,1}}
will be used to deal with the boundedness of the operator
\mathcal{G}
.

We define the operator
\mathcal{F}
as:

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}(\rho,U)=\Big(\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U),\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho,U)\Big),

(2.7)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U)=G*_{(t,x)}(\mathcal{I}(\rho,U)+\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)+A(U))+\mathcal{I}(\rho,U)+\mathcal{R}(\rho,U),

\displaystyle\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho,U)=(1-\Delta)^{-1}(\rho+A(U)).

We need to prove that
\mathcal{F}
has a fixed point i.e.
\mathcal{F}(\rho,U)=(\rho,U)
.

We define the norm

\displaystyle\|(g_{1},g_{2})\|_{S^{\varepsilon}_{\kappa}}:=\|g_{1}\|_{\kappa}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|g_{2}\|_{\kappa},~~~\|(g_{1},g_{2})\|_{l,\kappa}:=\|g_{1}\|_{l,\kappa}+\|g_{2}\|_{l,\kappa}.

The main Theorem
1.2
is a consequence of the following theorems:

Theorem 2.2

Let
a\in(\frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2},1)
. There exist constants
C_{0}>0
and
\widetilde{\varepsilon}>0
such that for any
\varepsilon\in(0,\widetilde{\varepsilon}]
we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}(\rho,U)\|_{S^{\varepsilon}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon,~~~~~~\|\mathcal{F}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{F}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{\varepsilon}_{a}}\leq\frac{1}{2}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{\varepsilon}_{a}},

for any
(\rho,U),(\rho_{1},U_{1}),(\rho_{2},U_{2})\in\mathfrak{B}(\varepsilon):=\{\rho,U\in L^{\infty}((0,\infty);L^{1}\cap L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d})):\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{\varepsilon}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon\}
, provided that

\sum_{i=0,1}\sum_{p=1,\infty}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{a}(\nabla^{i}_{x,v}f_{0})\right\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}}\leq\frac{1}{C_{0}}\varepsilon.

(2.8)

Under condition (
2.8
),
\mathcal{F}
has a unique fixed point
(\rho,U)
in
\mathfrak{B}(\varepsilon)
with
\|(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},a,M^{*}}1.

Moreover, set
\tilde{\varrho}_{0}(t,x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}f_{0}(x-tv,v)dv
, we have

\displaystyle\|(\rho-\tilde{\varrho}_{0}-G*_{(t,x)}\tilde{\varrho}_{0},U)\|_{S^{\varepsilon}_{a}}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M^{*},a}\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}}.

Theorem 2.3

Let
\rho
be in Theorem
2.2
. Then for
b\in(0,1)
and
m\geq 2
, we have

\|(\rho,U)\|_{m,b}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},a,M^{*},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime},C_{A,m}}1,

provided the initial data
f_{0}
, the stationary state
\mu
and function
A
satisfying

\begin{split}&\sum_{j=2}^{m+1}\sup_{|r|\leq 1}\left|{A^{(j)}(r)}\right|\leq C_{A,m},~~~\sum_{j=0}^{m}\sum_{p=1,\infty}\|\mathcal{D}^{a}(\nabla_{x,v}^{j}f_{0})\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}}\leq\mathbf{c}^{\prime},\\
&\|\langle v\rangle^{d+\gamma+5}\nabla\mu(\cdot)\|_{W^{2d+2\gamma+7,1}}+\langle v\rangle^{N}\sum_{j=0}^{m+3}|\nabla^{j}\mu(v)|\leq\mathbf{c}^{\prime}.\end{split}

(2.9)

3
Estimate of the kernel G and boundedness of operator
\mathcal{G}

In this section, we prove the boundedness of the operator
\mathcal{G}:\mathbf{f}\mapsto{G}*_{(t,x)}\mathbf{f}
under the norm
\|\cdot\|_{\gamma,a}
, where
\gamma\geq 0
. The main result is

\|\mathcal{G}(\mathbf{f})\|_{\gamma,a}\leq M\|\mathbf{f}\|_{\gamma,a},

for some constant

M=M(\text{\r{c}},d,M_{\gamma+1})
.

To do this, we need the following Lemmas:

Lemma 3.1

Define

Q_{0}(u,\tau,\xi)=\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-i\tau t}u(t\xi)dt.

Then,

\displaystyle|Q_{0}(u,\tau,\xi)|\lesssim\frac{\sup_{x}\left(|u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2}\right)}{|\xi|},

(3.1)

\displaystyle|Q_{0}(u,\tau,\xi)|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{m-1}\frac{|\xi|^{j}}{|\tau|^{j+1}}|\nabla^{j}u(0)|+\frac{|\xi|^{m-1}}{|\tau|^{m}}\sup_{x\in\mathbb{R}}\left(|\nabla^{m}u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2}\right).

(3.2)

Proof.
Firstly, we can get the result (
3.1
)
directly as follows,

\displaystyle|Q_{0}(u,\tau,\xi)|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{+\infty}|u(t\xi)|dt\leq\sup_{z\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(|u(z)|\langle z\rangle^{2}\right)\int_{0}^{+\infty}\frac{1}{\langle t\xi\rangle^{2}}dt\sim\sup_{z\in\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(|u(z)|\langle z\rangle^{2}\right)\frac{1}{|\xi|}.

(3.3)

Moreover, we integrate by parts m-times to obtain that for any nonnegative integer
m
,

\displaystyle Q_{0}(u,\tau,\xi)=-\left(\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}\frac{1}{(i\tau)^{j+1}}\xi^{\otimes j}:\nabla^{j}u(0)\right)+\frac{1}{(i\tau)^{m}}Q(\xi^{\otimes m}:\nabla^{m}u(t\xi),\tau,\xi).

Combining the above equality with (
3.3
) to get the conclusion (
3.2
).

Lemma 3.2

For
k\in\mathbb{Z}^{+}
, define

\displaystyle Q_{k}(u,\tau,\xi)=\int_{0}^{+\infty}t^{k}e^{-i\tau t}u(t\xi)dt.

(3.4)

Then,

\displaystyle|Q_{k}(u,\tau,\xi)|\lesssim\frac{\sup_{x}\left(|u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2+k}\right)}{|\xi|^{k+1}},

(3.5)

\displaystyle|Q_{k}(u,\tau,\xi)|\lesssim\frac{1}{|\tau|^{k+1}}\left(|u(0)|+\sum_{l=0}^{k}\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{k-l+1}u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2+k-l}\right)\right),

(3.6)

\displaystyle|Q_{k}(u,\tau,\xi)|\lesssim\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{k+1}}\left(\sup_{x}\left(|u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2+k}\right)+\sum_{l=0}^{k}\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{k-l+1}u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2+k-l}\right)\right).

(3.7)

Moreover, if
u(0)=0
, we have

\displaystyle|Q_{k}(u,\tau,\xi)|\lesssim\frac{|\xi|}{|\tau|^{k+2}}\sum_{l=0}^{k+1}\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{k-l+2}u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{3+k-l}\right),

(3.8)

\displaystyle|Q_{k}(u,\tau,\xi)|\lesssim\frac{|\xi|}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{k+2}}\left(\sup_{x}\left(|u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2+k}\right)+\sum_{l=0}^{k+1}\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{k-l+2}u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{3+k-l}\right)\right).

(3.9)

Proof.
First of all,
\forall j\in\{1,2,...,d\}
, we obtain

\xi_{j}^{k}Q_{k}(u,\tau,\xi)=\int_{0}^{+\infty}(t\xi_{j})^{k}e^{-i\tau t}u(t\xi)dt=Q_{0}(u_{1},\tau,\xi),

where

u_{1}(\xi)=\xi_{j}^{k}u(\xi).

By (
3.1
), one has,

|\xi_{j}^{k}Q_{k}(u,\tau,\xi)|\lesssim\frac{\sup_{x}\left(|u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2+k}\right)}{|\xi|},

which implies (
3.5
).

Note that

\displaystyle\nabla^{l}u_{1}(0)=0,\quad\forall l\leq k-1,~~~~\quad|\nabla^{l}u_{1}(0)|\lesssim|\nabla^{l-k}u(0)|,\quad\forall l\geq k.

Then, we know from (
3.2
) with
m=k+1
that

\displaystyle|\xi_{j}^{k}Q_{k}(u,\tau,\xi)|=Q_{0}(u_{1},\tau,\xi)\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\xi|^{k}}{|\tau|^{k+1}}|\nabla^{k}u_{1}(0)|+\frac{|\xi|^{k}}{|\tau|^{k+1}}\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{k+1}u_{1}(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\xi|^{k}}{|\tau|^{k+1}}|u(0)|+\frac{|\xi|^{k}}{|\tau|^{k+1}}\sum_{l=0}^{k}\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{k-l+1}u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2+k-l}\right).

Then,

|\xi|^{k}|Q_{k}(u,\tau,\xi)|\lesssim\frac{|\xi|^{k}}{|\tau|^{k+1}}|u(0)|+\frac{|\xi|^{k}}{|\tau|^{k+1}}\sum_{l=0}^{k}\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{k-l+1}u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2+k-l}\right),

which implies (
3.6
).

Then (
3.5
) and (
3.6
) imply (
3.7
) directly.

Moreover, if
u(0)=0
, thanks to (
3.2
) with
m=k+2
, we have

\displaystyle|\xi_{j}^{k}Q_{k}(u,\tau,\xi)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\xi|^{k+1}}{|\tau|^{k+2}}|\nabla^{k+1}u_{1}(0)|+\frac{|\xi|^{k+1}}{|\tau|^{k+2}}\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{k+2}u_{1}(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\xi|^{k+1}}{|\tau|^{k+2}}|\nabla u(0)|+\frac{|\xi|^{k+1}}{|\tau|^{k+2}}\sum_{l=0}^{k+1}\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{k-l+2}u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{3+k-l}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\xi|^{k+1}}{|\tau|^{k+2}}\sum_{l=0}^{k+1}\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{k-l+2}u(x)|\langle x\rangle^{3+k-l}\right),

which implies (
3.8
).
Finally, (
3.5
) and (
3.8
) imply (
3.9
).

We define

\|F\|_{\mathcal{W}^{\beta}_{\alpha}}:=\sum_{j=0}^{\beta}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\langle x\rangle^{\alpha}|\nabla^{j}F(x)|dx,

and use this norm in the following lemmas.

Lemma 3.3

For integer
\alpha\geq 0
and
\beta\geq 2
, we have following estimates:

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{\xi}\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)\right|\lesssim\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2}}\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}^{3}_{3}},

(3.10)

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{\xi}^{\beta}\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)\right|\lesssim\left(\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{\beta}}\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2}}+\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{\beta}}\right)\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}^{2\beta+1}_{\beta+2}},

(3.11)

\displaystyle\left|\partial^{\alpha}_{\tau}\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)\right|\lesssim\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{\alpha+2}}\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}_{\alpha+3}^{\alpha+2}}.

(3.12)

Proof.
Recall that

\displaystyle\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)=\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-i\tau t}\frac{1}{1+|\xi|^{2}}i\xi\cdot\widehat{\nabla_{v}\mu}(t\xi)dt.

(3.13)

Then for
\alpha\geq 0
, we have

\partial^{\alpha}_{\tau}\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)=\sum_{j=1}^{d}\frac{(-i)^{\alpha}\xi_{j}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\int_{0}^{+\infty}t^{\alpha}e^{-i\tau t}P_{j}(t\xi)dt,

where

P(\xi)=(P_{1},P_{2},...,P_{d})(\xi)=\widehat{\nabla_{v}\mu}(\xi),\quad P(0)=0.

Thanks to (
3.9
), we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}t^{\alpha}e^{-i\tau t}P_{j}(t\xi)dt\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\xi|}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{\alpha+2}}\left(\sup_{x}\left(|P(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2+\alpha}\right)+\sum_{l=0}^{\alpha+1}\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{\alpha-l+2}P(x)|\langle x\rangle^{3+\alpha-l}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{|\xi|}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{\alpha+2}}\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}_{\alpha+3}^{\alpha+2}}.

Thus we obtain that

\displaystyle\left|\partial^{\alpha}_{\tau}\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{d}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}t^{\alpha}e^{-i\tau t}P_{j}(t\xi)dt\right|\lesssim\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{\alpha+2}}\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}_{\alpha+3}^{\alpha+2}}.

Thanks to (
3.7
) with
k=\beta_{1}
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}t^{\beta_{1}}e^{-i\tau t}(\nabla^{\beta_{1}}P_{j})(t\xi)dt\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{\beta_{1}+1}}\left(\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{\beta_{1}}P(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2+\beta_{1}}\right)+\sum_{l=0}^{\beta_{1}}\sup_{x}\left(|\nabla^{2\beta_{1}-l+1}P(x)|\langle x\rangle^{2+\beta_{1}-l}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{\beta_{1}+1}}\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}^{2\beta_{1}+1}_{\beta_{1}+2}}.

Therefore,

\left|\nabla_{\xi}\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)\right|\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{d}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}te^{-i\tau t}(\nabla P_{j})(t\xi)dt\right|+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-i\tau t}P_{j}(t\xi)dt\right|\lesssim\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2}}\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}^{3}_{3}}.

For
\beta\geq 2
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{\xi}^{\beta}\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{d}\sum_{\beta_{1}=0}^{\beta}\left|\nabla_{\xi}^{\beta-\beta_{1}}\left(\frac{\xi_{j}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\right)\right|\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}t^{\beta_{1}}e^{-i\tau t}(\nabla^{\beta_{1}}P_{j})(t\xi)dt\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{d}\sum_{\beta_{1}=1}^{\beta-1}\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{\beta-\beta_{1}+1}}\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}t^{\beta_{1}}e^{-i\tau t}(\nabla^{\beta_{1}}P_{j})(t\xi)dt\right|

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}t^{\beta}e^{-i\tau t}(\nabla^{\beta}P_{j})(t\xi)dt\right|+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{\beta+1}}\left|\int_{0}^{+\infty}e^{-i\tau t}P_{j}(t\xi)dt\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}^{2\beta+1}_{\beta+2}}\left(\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{\beta}}\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2}}+\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{\beta}}\right).

Thus, the proof is complete.

Lemma 3.4

For any integer
j\geq 1
, there holds

\displaystyle\left|\nabla^{j}\left(\frac{\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\right)\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{j}\left(\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{j}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2}}+\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{j}}\right),

(3.14)

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{\tau}^{j}\left(\frac{\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\right)\right|\lesssim

\displaystyle M_{j}\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{j+2}},

(3.15)

where

M_{j}=\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}_{j+2}^{2j+1}}\left(1+\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}_{j+2}^{2j+1}}\right)^{j-1}.

Proof.
Thanks to the Penrose condition, one has
|1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)|\geq\text{\r{c}}
.
Firstly, we have

\left|\nabla\frac{\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\right|=\left|\nabla\left(\frac{1}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\right)\right|\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}|\nabla\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)|\overset{\eqref{alpha and beta K estimate}}{\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}}\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2}}\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}^{3}_{3}}.

Note that we get the following lines for any
j\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\left|\nabla^{j}\left(\frac{\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\right)\right|=\left|\nabla^{j}\left(\frac{1}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\right)\right|\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\sum_{i=1}^{j}|\nabla^{i}\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)|^{\frac{j}{i}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\sum_{i=2}^{j}\left(\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{j}}\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{\frac{2j}{i}}}+\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{\frac{2j}{i}}}\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{j}}\right)\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}^{2i+1}_{i+2}}^{\frac{j}{i}}+\ \frac{|\xi|^{j}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2j}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2j}}\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}^{3}_{3}}^{j}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\left(\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{j}}\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2}}+\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{j}}\right)\left(\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}_{j+2}^{2j+1}}+\|\nabla\mu\|^{j}_{\mathcal{W}_{j+2}^{2j+1}}\right)+\ \frac{|\xi|^{j}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2j}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2j}}\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}^{3}_{3}}^{j}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}M_{j}\left(\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{j}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2}}+\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{j}}\right).

Moreover, one has

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{\tau}^{j}\left(\frac{\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\right)\right|=

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{\tau}^{j}\left(\frac{1}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\right)\right|\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{j}\left|\partial_{\tau}^{j_{1}}\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)\right|^{\frac{j}{j_{1}}}\overset{\eqref{alpha K estimate}}{\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}}\sum_{j_{1}=1}^{j}\left|\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{j_{1}+2}}\right|^{\frac{j}{j_{1}}}\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}_{j_{1}+3}^{j_{1}+2}}^{\frac{j}{j_{1}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{j}}\left(\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2}}+\frac{|\xi|^{2j}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2j}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2j}}\right)\left(\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}^{j+2}_{j+3}}^{j}+\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}^{j+2}_{j+3}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle M_{j}\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{j+2}}.

This completes the proof.

Lemma 3.5

Define
G_{n}=\mathscr{F}^{-1}\left(\widetilde{G}\chi_{n}\right),

where
\chi\in[0,1]
is a smooth compactly supported function in the annulus
1/4\leq|\xi|\leq 4
and
\chi_{n}(\xi)=\chi(\frac{\xi}{2^{n}})
.
Then, we have for any integer
\gamma\geq 0

\left|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)\right|\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}2^{(d+\gamma+1)n-2n^{+}}M_{d+\gamma+2}\min\left\{1,\frac{1}{2^{n}(t+|x|)}\right\}^{d+\gamma+2},

where
n^{+}=\max\{0,n\}
.

Proof.
Firstly, we estimate
|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)|
directly.

\displaystyle|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)|=

\displaystyle\left|\mathscr{F}^{-1}\left(\xi^{\otimes\gamma}\frac{\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\chi_{n}(\xi)\right)\right|\lesssim\int_{\tau\in\mathbb{R},|\xi|\sim 2^{n}}\left|\left(\frac{\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\right)\xi^{\otimes\gamma}\chi_{n}(\xi)\right|d\xi d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\int_{\tau\in\mathbb{R},|\xi|\sim 2^{n}}2^{\gamma n}|\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)|d\tau d\xi.

Using (
3.12
), one has

\displaystyle|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)|\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}_{3}^{2}}\int_{\tau\in\mathbb{R},|\xi|\sim 2^{n}}2^{\gamma n}\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2}}d\tau d\xi\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}2^{(d+\gamma+1)n-2n^{+}}\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}_{3}^{2}}.

(3.16)

Then we integrate by parts in
\tau
to get that

\displaystyle|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)|\sim

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}}e^{i\tau t}e^{i\xi\cdot x}\frac{\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\xi^{\otimes\gamma}\chi_{n}(\xi)d\xi d\tau\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|t|^{m_{1}}}\int_{\tau\in\mathbb{R},|\xi|\sim 2^{n}}\left|\partial_{\tau}^{m_{1}}\left(\frac{\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\right)\xi^{\otimes\gamma}\chi_{n}(\xi)\right|d\xi d\tau.

Using (
3.15
), we obtain that

\displaystyle|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)|\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle M_{m_{1}}\frac{1}{|t|^{m_{1}}}\int_{\tau\in\mathbb{R},|\xi|\sim 2^{n}}\frac{|\xi|^{2+\gamma}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{m_{1}+2}}d\xi d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(|t|2^{n})^{m_{1}}}2^{(d+\gamma+1)n-2n^{+}}M_{m_{1}}.

(3.17)

Finally, we integrate by parts in
\xi
to get that

\displaystyle|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)|\sim

\displaystyle\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}}e^{i\tau t}e^{i\xi\cdot x}\frac{\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\xi^{\otimes\gamma}\chi_{n}(\xi)d\xi d\tau\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|x|^{m_{2}}}\int_{\tau\in\mathbb{R},|\xi|\sim 2^{n}}\left|\nabla^{m_{2}}\left(\frac{\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\xi^{\otimes\gamma}\chi_{n}(\xi)\right)\right|d\xi d\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|x|^{m_{2}}}\int_{\tau\in\mathbb{R},|\xi|\sim 2^{n}}\left|\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)\right|\left|\nabla^{m_{2}}\left(\xi^{\otimes\gamma}\chi_{n}(\xi)\right)\right|d\xi d\tau

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{|x|^{m_{2}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m_{2}}\int_{\tau\in\mathbb{R},|\xi|\sim 2^{n}}\left|\nabla^{j}\frac{\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}{1-\widetilde{K}(\tau,\xi)}\right|\left|\nabla^{m_{2}-j}\left(\xi^{\otimes\gamma}\chi_{n}(\xi)\right)\right|d\xi d\tau.

Using (
3.10
) and (
3.14
), we obtain that

\begin{split}|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)|\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}&\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}_{3}^{2}}\frac{1}{|x|^{m_{2}}}\int_{\tau\in\mathbb{R},|\xi|\sim 2^{n}}\frac{|\xi|^{2}}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2}}2^{(\gamma-m_{2})n}d\xi d\tau\\
&+\frac{1}{|x|^{m_{2}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m_{2}}\int_{\tau\in\mathbb{R},|\xi|\sim 2^{n}}M_{j}\left(\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{j}}\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{2}}+\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\frac{1}{(|\tau|+|\xi|)^{j}}\right)2^{(\gamma-m_{2}+j)n}d\xi d\tau\\
\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}&\frac{M_{m_{2}}}{|x|^{m_{2}}}\sum_{j=1}^{m_{2}}\int_{|\xi|\sim 2^{n}}\left[\frac{|\xi|}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}2^{(\gamma-m_{2})n}+\left(\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{j}}\frac{1}{|\xi|}+\frac{1}{\langle\xi\rangle^{2}}\frac{1}{|\xi|^{j-1}}\right)2^{(\gamma-m_{2}+j)n}\right]d\xi\\
\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}&\frac{1}{(|x|2^{n})^{m_{2}}}2^{(d+\gamma+1)n-2n^{+}}M_{m_{2}}.\end{split}

(3.18)

Combining (
3.16
), (
3.17
) with (
3.18
) and taking
m_{1}=m_{2}=d+\gamma+2
, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)\right|\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}2^{(d+\gamma+1)n-2n^{+}}M_{d+\gamma+2}\min\left\{1,\frac{1}{2^{n}(t+|x|)}\right\}^{d+\gamma+2}.

This gives the result.

Theorem 3.6

Let
\gamma\geq 0
,
0\leq a_{0}<1
and
0<b_{0}<1
. Then,

|\nabla^{\gamma}G(t,x)|\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}}{(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma-1}(1+t+|x|)^{2}}.

(3.19)

In particular,

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{\gamma}G(t,x)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}}{t^{d+\gamma-1}(1+t)^{2}},

(3.20)

\displaystyle\||x|^{a_{0}}\nabla^{\gamma}G(t,x)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}}{t^{\gamma-1-a_{0}}(1+t)^{2}}+\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\mathbf{1}_{t\leq 1}+\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\mathbf{1}_{\gamma=1+a_{0}}\log^{+}\left(\frac{1}{t}\right),

(3.21)

\displaystyle\||x|^{b_{0}}\delta_{\alpha}G(t,x)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\widetilde{M}_{1}\frac{|\alpha|}{(1+t+|\alpha|)^{2-b_{0}}}+\widetilde{M}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|>t}\frac{|\alpha|^{b_{0}}}{1+t}+\widetilde{M}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\leq t}\frac{|\alpha|^{d+b_{0}}}{t^{d-1}(1+t)^{2}},

(3.22)

where

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}=\overline{M}_{\gamma}\big(1+\overline{M}_{\gamma}\big)^{d+\gamma+1},\qquad\text{and}\quad\overline{M}_{\gamma}=\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{d+\gamma+4}\nabla\mu(\cdot)\|_{W^{2d+2\gamma+5,1}}.

Proof.
Firstly, we prove (
3.19
).
Note that

\|\nabla\mu\|_{\mathcal{W}_{\alpha}^{\beta}}\lesssim\|\langle\cdot\rangle^{\alpha}\nabla\mu\|_{W^{\beta,1}},

thus

M_{d+\gamma+2}\lesssim\overline{M}_{\gamma}\big(1+\overline{M}_{\gamma}\big)^{d+\gamma+1}=\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}.

Since

\displaystyle|\nabla^{\gamma}G(t,x)|\leq\sum_{n\leq 0}\left|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)\right|+\sum_{n>0}\left|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)\right|,

for
n>0
, combining this with Lemma
3.5
, we can yield that

\displaystyle\sum_{n>0}\left|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)\right|\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\sum_{n>0}2^{(d+\gamma-1)n}\min\left\{1,\frac{1}{2^{n}(t+|x|)}\right\}^{d+\gamma+2}

\displaystyle\sim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\sum_{n>0}\Bigg(\mathbf{1}_{t+|x|\leq 1}\sum_{1\leq 2^{n}\leq(t+|x|)^{-1}}2^{(d+\gamma-1)n}+\mathbf{1}_{t+|x|\leq 1}\sum_{2^{n}>(t+|x|)^{-1}}2^{-3n}\frac{1}{(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma+2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\mathbf{1}_{t+|x|>1}\frac{1}{2^{3n}(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma+2}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\left(\mathbf{1}_{t+|x|\leq 1}\frac{1}{(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma-1}}+\mathbf{1}_{t+|x|>1}\frac{1}{(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma+2}}\right).

Similarly, we have for
n\leq 0
,

\displaystyle\sum_{n\leq 0}\left|\nabla^{\gamma}G_{n}(t,x)\right|\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\left(\mathbf{1}_{t+|x|\leq 1}\sum_{n\leq 0}2^{(d+\gamma+1)n}+\mathbf{1}_{t+|x|>1}\sum_{2^{n}\leq(t+|x|)^{-1}}2^{(d+\gamma+1)n}\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\mathbf{1}_{t+|x|>1}\sum_{(t+|x|)^{-1}<2^{n}\leq 1}\frac{2^{-n}}{(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma+2}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\left(\mathbf{1}_{t+|x|\leq 1}+\mathbf{1}_{t+|x|>1}\frac{1}{(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma+1}}\right).

Finally, we deduce that

\displaystyle|\nabla^{\gamma}G(t,x)|\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\left(\mathbf{1}_{t+|x|\leq 1}\frac{1}{(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma-1}}+\mathbf{1}_{t+|x|>1}\frac{1}{(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma+1}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\frac{\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}}{(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma-1}(1+t+|x|)^{2}}.

This implies (
3.19
) and (
3.20
).

(1)
Now we prove (
3.21
). One has,

\displaystyle\||x|^{a_{0}}\nabla^{\gamma}G(t,x)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\int\frac{|x|^{a_{0}}}{(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma-1}(1+t+|x|)^{2}}dx.

If
t\geq 1
, we estimate

\displaystyle\||x|^{a_{0}}\nabla^{\gamma}G(t,x)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\int\frac{|x|^{a_{0}}}{(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma+1}}dx\sim_{\text{\r{c}}}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}}{t^{\gamma+1-a_{0}}}\int\frac{|x|^{a_{0}}}{(1+|x|)^{d+\gamma+1}}dx\sim_{\text{\r{c}}}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}}{t^{\gamma+1-a_{0}}}.

If
0\leq t\leq 1
, we estimate

\displaystyle\||x|^{a_{0}}\nabla^{\gamma}G(t,x)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\left(\int_{|x|\leq t}+\int_{t<|x|<1}+\int_{|x|\geq 1}\right)\frac{|x|^{a_{0}}}{(t+|x|)^{d+\gamma-1}(1+t+|x|)^{2}}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\left(\int_{|x|\leq t}\frac{|x|^{a_{0}}}{t^{d+\gamma-1}}dx+\int_{t<|x|<1}\frac{|x|^{a_{0}}}{|x|^{d+\gamma-1}}dx+\int_{|x|\geq 1}\frac{|x|^{a_{0}}}{|x|^{d+\gamma+1}}dx\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\gamma-1-{a_{0}}}}+1+\mathbf{1}_{\gamma<1+a_{0}}+\mathbf{1}_{\gamma=1+a_{0}}\log\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)+\frac{\mathbf{1}_{\gamma>1+a_{0}}}{t^{\gamma-a_{0}-1}}\right)

\displaystyle\sim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\gamma-1-a_{0}}}+1+\mathbf{1}_{\gamma=1+a_{0}}\log\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)\right).

Combining these two cases to deduce that

\displaystyle\||x|^{a_{0}}\nabla^{\gamma}G(t,x)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\mathbf{1}_{t\geq 1}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}}{t^{\gamma+1-a_{0}}}+\mathbf{1}_{t\leq 1}\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\left(\frac{1}{t^{\gamma-1-a_{0}}}+1+\mathbf{1}_{\gamma=1+a_{0}}\log\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\sim_{\text{\r{c}}}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}}{t^{\gamma-1-a_{0}}(1+t)^{2}}+\widetilde{M}_{\gamma}\mathbf{1}_{t\leq 1}+\mathbf{1}_{\gamma=1+a_{0}}\log^{+}\left(\frac{1}{t}\right).

(2)
Now we prove (
3.22
). One has

\displaystyle\||x|^{b_{0}}|\delta_{\alpha}G(t,x)|\|_{L^{1}_{x}}=\left(\int_{|\alpha|\leq|x|/2}+\int_{|\alpha|\geq|x|/2}\right)|x|^{b_{0}}|\delta_{\alpha}G(t,x)|dx=:

\displaystyle I_{G,b_{0}}^{1}+I_{G,b_{0}}^{2}.

For the term
I^{1}_{G,b_{0}}
,

\displaystyle I^{1}_{G,b_{0}}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{|\alpha|\leq|x|/2}\int_{0}^{1}|x|^{b_{0}}\left|\nabla G(t,x-\tau\alpha)\right||\alpha|d\tau dx

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle|\alpha|\int_{0}^{1}\int_{|\alpha|\leq|x|/2}|x|^{b_{0}}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{1}}{(t+|x-\tau\alpha|)^{d}(1+t+|x-\tau\alpha|)^{2}}dxd\tau

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle|\alpha|\int_{|\alpha|\leq|x|/2}|x|^{b_{0}}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{1}}{(t+|x|/2)^{d}(1+t+|x|/2)^{2}}dx

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\geq t/2}|\alpha|\int_{|\alpha|\leq|x|/2}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{1}}{|x|^{d-b_{0}}(1+|x|)^{2}}dx+\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|<t/2}|\alpha|\int_{|\alpha|\leq|x|/2}|x|^{b_{0}}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{1}}{(t+|x|/2)^{d}(1+t+|x|/2)^{2}}dx

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle:I^{1,1}_{G,b_{0}}+I^{1,2}_{G,b_{0}}.

Then, we estimate that

\displaystyle I^{1,1}_{G,b_{0}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\geq t/2}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\geq 1}|\alpha|\int_{|\alpha|\leq|x|/2}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{1}}{|x|^{d+2-b_{0}}}dx

\displaystyle+\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\geq t/2}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|<1}|\alpha|\left(\int_{|\alpha|\leq|x|\leq 2}\widetilde{M}_{1}\frac{1}{|x|^{d-b_{0}}}dx+\int_{|x|\geq 2}\widetilde{M}_{1}\frac{1}{|x|^{d+2-b_{0}}}dx\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{1}|\alpha|\left(\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\geq t/2}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\geq 1}\frac{1}{|\alpha|^{2-b_{0}}}+\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\geq t/2}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|<1}\right)\sim\widetilde{M}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\geq t/2}\frac{|\alpha|}{(1+|\alpha|)^{2-b_{0}}},

(3.23)

and

\begin{split}I^{1,2}_{G,b_{0}}\lesssim&\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|<t/2}|\alpha|\int_{|x|\geq t}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{1}}{|x|^{d-b_{0}}(1+|x|)^{2}}dx+\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|<t/2}|\alpha|\int_{2|\alpha|\leq|x|\leq t}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{1}|x|^{b_{0}}}{t^{d}(1+t)^{2}}dx\\
\lesssim&\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|<t/2}\mathbf{1}_{t\geq 1}|\alpha|\int_{|x|\geq t}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{1}}{|x|^{d-b_{0}+2}}dx+\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|<t/2}\mathbf{1}_{t\leq 1}|\alpha|\left(\int_{t\leq|x|\leq 1}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{1}}{|x|^{d-b_{0}}}dx+\int_{|x|\geq 1}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{1}}{|x|^{d-b_{0}+2}}dx\right)\\
&+\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|<t/2}|\alpha|\int_{|\alpha|\leq|x|\leq 2t}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{1}|x|^{b_{0}}}{t^{d}(1+t)^{2}}dx\\
\sim&\widetilde{M}_{1}|\alpha|\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|<t/2}\left(\mathbf{1}_{t\geq 1}\frac{1}{t^{2-b_{0}}}+\mathbf{1}_{t\leq 1}+\frac{t^{b_{0}}}{(1+t)^{2}}\right)\sim\widetilde{M}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|<t}\frac{|\alpha|}{(1+t)^{2-b_{0}}}.\end{split}

(3.24)

Combining (
3.23
) with (
3.24
) to yield

I_{G,b_{0}}^{1}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\widetilde{M}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\geq t}\frac{|\alpha|}{(1+|\alpha|)^{2-b_{0}}}+\widetilde{M}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|<t}\frac{|\alpha|}{(1+t)^{2-b_{0}}}\sim_{\text{\r{c}}}\widetilde{M}_{1}\frac{|\alpha|}{(1+t+|\alpha|)^{2-b_{0}}}.

(3.25)

For the term
I_{G,b_{0}}^{2}
, one has

\displaystyle I_{G,b_{0}}^{2}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{0}|\alpha|^{b_{0}}\int_{|x|\lesssim|\alpha|}\frac{1}{(t+|x|)^{d-1}(1+t+|x|)^{2}}dx\sim_{\text{\r{c}}}\widetilde{M}_{0}|\alpha|^{b_{0}}\int_{0}^{|\alpha|}\frac{r^{d-1}}{(t+r)^{d-1}(1+t+r)^{2}}dr

\displaystyle\sim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|>t}\widetilde{M}_{0}|\alpha|^{b_{0}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\frac{r^{d-1}}{t^{d-1}(1+t)^{2}}dr+\int_{t}^{|\alpha|}\frac{r^{d-1}}{r^{d-1}(1+r)^{2}}dr\right)+\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\leq t}\widetilde{M}_{0}|\alpha|^{b_{0}}\int_{0}^{|\alpha|}\frac{r^{d-1}}{t^{d-1}(1+t)^{2}}dr

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|>t}\widetilde{M}_{0}|\alpha|^{b_{0}}\left(\frac{t}{(1+t)^{2}}+\frac{1}{1+t}\right)+\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\leq t}\frac{\widetilde{M}_{0}|\alpha|^{d+b_{0}}}{t^{d-1}(1+t)^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}_{0}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|>t}\frac{|\alpha|^{b_{0}}}{1+t}+\widetilde{M}_{0}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\leq t}\frac{|\alpha|^{d+b_{0}}}{t^{d-1}(1+t)^{2}}.

Combining this with (
3.25
) and note that
\widetilde{M}_{0}\leq\widetilde{M}_{1}
, one gets

\displaystyle\||x|^{b_{0}}\delta_{\alpha}G(t,x)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}\widetilde{M}_{1}\frac{|\alpha|}{(1+t+|\alpha|)^{2-b_{0}}}+\widetilde{M}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|>t}\frac{|\alpha|^{b_{0}}}{1+t}+\widetilde{M}_{1}\mathbf{1}_{|\alpha|\leq t}\frac{|\alpha|^{d+b_{0}}}{t^{d-1}(1+t)^{2}},

which implies (
3.22
).

Lemma 3.7

For the kernel
G
defined in (
2.6
),
there holds

\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}G(t,x)dx=0,~~\forall t\geq 0.

(3.26)

Proof.
By (
3.12
) with
\alpha=0
, one has
\widetilde{K}(\tau,0)=0
.
Then from (
2.6
) , we obtain that
\widetilde{G}(\tau,0)=0
, for all
\tau\in\mathbb{R}
, which implies the conclusion (
3.26
).

Then we can prove the main result in this section.

Theorem 3.8

There holds

\|G*_{(t,x)}\mathbf{f}\|_{\gamma,a}\leq M\|\mathbf{f}\|_{\gamma,a},

with

M=M(\text{\r{c}},d,M_{\gamma+1})
.

It is easy to check that for any
t\in[0,1]

\sum_{p=1,\infty}\left(\|G*_{(t,x)}\mathbf{f}(t)\|_{L^{p}}+\|\nabla^{\gamma}(G*_{(t,x)}\mathbf{f}(t))\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\right)\leq\|\mathbf{f}\|_{\gamma,a}\int_{0}^{1}\|G(s)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}ds\overset{\eqref{Z3}}{\lesssim}_{\text{\r{c}},d,M_{\gamma+1}}\|\mathbf{f}\|_{\gamma,a}.

To prove the theorem
3.8
, we need to have the following Lemmas.

Lemma 3.9

Let
t\geq 1
,
and
p=1,\infty
, we have

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|G*_{(t,x)}\mathbf{f}(t)\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{0}}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\left(\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{L^{1}}+\langle s\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\right).

Proof.
We use the idea in
[
11
]
.
Since we have the cancellation shown in the (
3.26
), we rewrite lines as follows when changing of variable
z=x-y
in the second term,

\displaystyle\|G*_{(t,x)}\mathbf{f}\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\leq\left\|\int_{0}^{t/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}G(t-s,x-y)\mathbf{f}(s,y)dyds\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\|\int_{t/2}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}G(t-s,z)(\mathbf{f}(s,x)-\mathbf{f}(s,x+z))dzds\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}:=I_{1}+I_{2}.

First, we consider the term
I_{1},

I_{1}\leq\int_{0}^{t/2}\|G(t-s)\|_{L^{p}}\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{L^{1}}ds.

Using (
3.20
) and (
3.21
) in Theorem
3.6
, we obtain

\displaystyle I_{1}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{0}}\int_{0}^{t/2}\frac{1}{\langle t-s\rangle^{1+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{L^{1}}ds\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{0}}\widetilde{M}{\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{L^{1}}.

(3.27)

Similarly, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}I_{2}&=\left\|\int_{t/2}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}G(t-s,z)|z|^{a}\frac{\mathbf{f}(s,x)-\mathbf{f}(s,x+z)}{|z|^{a}}dzds\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\\
&\leq\int_{t/2}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|G(t-s,z)||z|^{a}\frac{\left\|\mathbf{f}(s,x)-\mathbf{f}(s,x+z)\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}}{|z|^{a}}dzds\\
&\leq\int_{t/2}^{t}\sup_{z}\frac{\left\|\delta_{z}\mathbf{f}(s,x)\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}}{|z|^{a}}\|G(t-s,z)|z|^{a}\|_{L^{1}_{x}}ds.\end{split}

(3.28)

Using (
3.21
) in Theorem
3.6
, we have,

\displaystyle I_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{0}}\widetilde{M}\int_{t/2}^{t}\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\frac{1}{\langle t-s\rangle^{1-a}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{0}}\widetilde{M}\left[\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a}\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\right]\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}-a}\int_{t/2}^{t}\frac{1}{\langle t-s\rangle^{1-a}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{0}}\widetilde{M}\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\left(\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a}\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\right).

Combining (
3.27
) and (
3.28
) we obtain that

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|G*_{(t,x)}\mathbf{f}(t)\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{0}}\widetilde{M}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\left(\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{L^{1}}+\langle s\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|\mathbf{f}\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\right).

This implies the result.

Lemma 3.10

Let
t\geq 1
,
and
p=1,\infty
, we have estimates as follows,

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)+\gamma}{p}}\|\nabla^{\gamma}(G*_{(t,x)}\mathbf{f}(t))\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{1}}\widetilde{M}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\left(\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{L^{1}}+\langle s\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\gamma}\|\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\right).

Proof.
When referring to
\|G*_{(t,x)}\mathbf{f}(t)\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}
, we consider
\|\delta_{\alpha}(G*_{(t,x)}\mathbf{f}(t))\|_{L^{p}}
at first.
Set

\displaystyle u_{1}(t,x)=\int_{0}^{t/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}G(t-s,y)\mathbf{f}(s,x-y)dyds,

\displaystyle u_{2}(t,x)=\int_{t/2}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}G(t-s,y)\mathbf{f}(s,x-y)dyds.

Thus,

\|\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{\gamma}(G*_{(t,x)}\mathbf{f}(t))\|_{L^{p}}\leq\|\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{\gamma}u_{1}(t,x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}+\|\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{\gamma}u_{2}(t,x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}.

Firstly, we focus on the second term. Thanks to (
3.21
) and (
3.26
), we have
for
|\alpha|>t/2\geq\frac{1}{2}

\displaystyle\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{\gamma}u_{2}(t,x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{2}{|\alpha|^{a}}\|\nabla^{\gamma}u_{2}(t,x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}=\frac{2}{|\alpha|^{a}}\left\|\int_{t/2}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}G(t-s,y)\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s,x-y)dyds\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{2}{|\alpha|^{a}}\int_{t/2}^{t}\||y|^{a}G(t-s,y)\|_{L^{1}_{y}}\sup_{y}\left(\frac{\|\delta_{y}\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{L^{p}}}{|y|^{a}}\right)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\alpha|^{a}}\left[\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a+\gamma}\|\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}\right]\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}-a-\gamma}\int_{t/2}^{t}\frac{\widetilde{M}}{\langle t-s\rangle^{1-a}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}\frac{\langle t\rangle^{a}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\left[\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a+\gamma}\|\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}\right]\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}-a-\gamma}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}

\displaystyle\widetilde{M}\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}-a-\gamma}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\left(\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a+\gamma}\|\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}\right).

For the case
|\alpha|\leq t/2
, we know that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{\gamma}u_{2}(t,x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{|\alpha|^{a}}\left\|\int_{t/2}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathbf{1}_{|t-s|\leq|\alpha|}\delta_{\alpha}G(t-s,y)\nabla^{\gamma}\delta_{y}\mathbf{f}(s,x)dyds\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{|\alpha|^{a}}\left\|\int_{t/2}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathbf{1}_{|t-s|>|\alpha|}\delta_{\alpha}G(t-s,y)\nabla^{\gamma}\delta_{y}\mathbf{f}(s,x)dyds\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}=:\mathcal{J}_{1}+\mathcal{J}_{2}.

For the term
\mathcal{J}_{1}
, one has

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|\alpha|^{a}}\left\|\int_{t/2}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathbf{1}_{|t-s|\leq|\alpha|}G(t-s,y)\delta_{y}\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s,x)dyds\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|\alpha|^{a}}\int_{t/2}^{t}\mathbf{1}_{|t-s|\leq|\alpha|}\||y|^{a}G(t-s,y)\|_{L^{1}}\sup_{y}\frac{\|\delta_{y}\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{L^{p}}}{|y|^{a}}ds

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{Z3}}{\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}}\frac{1}{|\alpha|^{a}}\left[\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a+\gamma}\|\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}\right]\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}-a-\gamma}\int_{0}^{t/2}\||y|^{a}G(s,y)\|_{L^{1}_{y}}\mathbf{1}_{|s|\leq|\alpha|}ds.

Since we have

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t/2}\||y|^{a}G(s,y)\|_{L^{1}_{y}}\mathbf{1}_{|s|\leq|\alpha|}ds\overset{\eqref{Z3}}{\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}}}}\widetilde{M}\int_{0}^{|\alpha|}\frac{1}{s^{1-a}}ds\leq\widetilde{M}|\alpha|^{a},

then

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{1}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{0}}\widetilde{M}\langle t\rangle^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}-a-\gamma}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\left(\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a+\gamma}\|\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}\right).

(3.29)

Now we deal with the term
\mathcal{J}_{2}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|\alpha|^{a}}\int_{t/2}^{t}\mathbf{1}_{|t-s|>|\alpha|}\left(\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|y|^{a}|\delta_{\alpha}G(t-s,y)|dy\right)\sup_{y}\frac{\left\|\delta_{y}\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s,x)\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}}{|y|^{a}}ds

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{|\alpha|^{a}}\langle t\rangle^{-a-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}-\gamma}\left[\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\langle s\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\gamma}\|\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\right]\int_{|\alpha|}^{t/2}\||y|^{a}\delta_{\alpha}G(s,y)\|_{L^{1}_{y}}ds

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{Z5}}{\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{0}}}\frac{1}{|\alpha|^{a}}\langle t\rangle^{-a-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}-\gamma}\left[\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\langle s\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\gamma}\|\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\right]\int_{|\alpha|}^{t/2}\frac{|\alpha|}{(1+s)^{2-a}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{0}}\langle t\rangle^{-a-\frac{d(p-1)-\gamma}{p}}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\left(\langle s\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\gamma}\|\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\right).

Combining this with (
3.29
) to conclude that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}u_{2}(t,x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{0}}\langle t\rangle^{-a-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}-\gamma}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\left(\langle s\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\gamma}\|\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\right).

Now we estimate
u_{1}(t,x)
. One

\displaystyle\|\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{\gamma}u_{1}(t,x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}=

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{0}^{t/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{\gamma}G(t-s,x-y)\mathbf{f}(s,y)dyds\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\|\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{\gamma}G(s)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}ds\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{L^{1}}.

Note that for any
s\gtrsim 1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{\gamma}G(s)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}

\displaystyle\leq\min\{\|\nabla^{\gamma}G(s)\|_{L^{p}_{x}},|\alpha|\|\nabla^{\gamma+1}G(s)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{Z2},\eqref{Z3}}{\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{\gamma+1}}}\min\left\{\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{1+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\gamma}},\frac{|\alpha|}{\langle s\rangle^{2+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\gamma}}\right\}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{\gamma+1}}\frac{|\alpha|^{a}}{\langle s\rangle^{1+a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\gamma}}.

Then

\displaystyle\int_{t/2}^{t}\|\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{\gamma}G(s)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{\gamma+1}}\frac{|\alpha|^{a}}{\langle t\rangle^{1+a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\gamma}}\langle t\rangle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{\gamma+1}}|\alpha|^{a}{\langle t\rangle^{-a-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}-\gamma}}.

Thus we obtain

\displaystyle\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{\gamma}u_{1}(t,x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{\gamma+1}}\langle t\rangle^{-a-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}-\gamma}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{L^{1}}.

Therefore, we deduce

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\gamma}\|G*_{(t,x)}\mathbf{f}(t)\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},M_{\gamma+1}}\sup_{s\in[0,\infty)}\left(\|\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{L^{1}}+\langle s\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\gamma}\|\nabla^{\gamma}\mathbf{f}(s)\|_{\dot{B}_{p,\infty}^{a}}\right).

This gives the result.

4
Estimates for Characteristics

In this section, we study the characteristics
(X_{s,t}(x,v),V_{s,t}(x,v))
defined in (
2.1
). First, we have for any
0\leq s\leq t<\infty
,

\begin{split}&X_{s,t}(x,v)=x-v(t-s)+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)E(\tau,X_{\tau,t}(x,v))d\tau,\\
&V_{s,t}(x,v)=v-\int_{s}^{t}E(\tau,X_{\tau,t}(x,v))d\tau.\end{split}

(4.1)

Set

\displaystyle\begin{split}&Y_{s,t}(x,v)=\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)E(\tau,x+\tau v+Y_{\tau,t}(x,v))d\tau,\\
&W_{s,t}(x,v)=-\int_{s}^{t}E(\tau,x+\tau v+Y_{\tau,t}(x,v))d\tau.\end{split}

(4.2)

Hence,

X_{s,t}(x,v)=x-(t-s)v+Y_{s,t}(x-vt,v),~~V_{s,t}(x,v)=v+W_{s,t}(x-vt,v).

(4.3)

We obtain the bounds on characteristics as follows.

Proposition 4.1

Let
a\in(\frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2},1)
, then there exists
\widetilde{\varepsilon_{0}}\in(0,1)
such that for any
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\leq\widetilde{\varepsilon_{0}}
and
0<\delta<a
, we have the following estimates:

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}\left(\|Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-3+a}\|\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\delta\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-3+\delta}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a-\delta}}\lesssim_{a}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}},

(4.4)

and

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-1+a}\left(\|W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad\quad+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}\|\nabla_{v}W_{s,t}(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-2}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\lesssim_{a}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

(4.5)

Here we use the notations

\displaystyle\delta_{\alpha}^{x}Y_{s,t}(x,v)=Y_{s,t}(x,v)-Y_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v),~~~\delta_{\alpha}^{v}Y_{s,t}(x,v)=Y_{s,t}(x,v)-Y_{s,t}(x,v-\alpha).

Before we prove this Proposition, we state the following Lemma:

Lemma 4.2

For
p=1,\infty
and
\kappa,\kappa_{1}\in(0,1)
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{1}\|\nabla^{j}(1-\Delta)^{-1}\psi\|_{L^{p}}+\|\nabla(1-\Delta)^{-1}\psi\|_{\dot{B}^{\kappa}_{p,\infty}}\leq c_{0}\|\psi\|_{L^{p}},

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{2}\|\nabla^{j}(1-\Delta)^{-1}\psi\|_{L^{p}}+[2+\kappa-(\kappa_{1}+j)]\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}j=0,1,2\\
\kappa_{1}+j<2+\kappa\end{subarray}}\|\nabla^{j}(1-\Delta)^{-1}\psi\|_{\dot{B}^{\kappa_{1}}_{p,\infty}\cap\dot{F}^{\kappa_{1}}_{p,\infty}}\leq c_{0}\|\psi\|_{\dot{B}^{\kappa}_{p,\infty}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{2}(1-\Delta)^{-1}\psi\|_{\dot{B}^{\kappa}_{\infty,\infty}}\leq c_{0}\|\psi\|_{\dot{B}^{\kappa}_{\infty,\infty}},

for some
c_{0}=c_{0}(\kappa)
.

Proof of Proposition
4.1
.

Note that
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}
implies
\|U\|_{a}\leq\varepsilon_{0}^{\frac{2}{3}}
, then by Assumption
3
, we have that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|A(U)\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}&\leq\max_{|r|\leq\varepsilon_{0}^{2/3}}|A^{\prime}(r)|\|U\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}\leq C_{A}\varepsilon_{0}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|U\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}},\\
\|A(U)\|_{L^{p}}&\leq C_{A}\|U^{2}\|_{L^{p}}\leq C_{A}\varepsilon_{0}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|U\|_{L^{p}}.\end{split}

(4.6)

Thus taking
\tilde{\varepsilon}_{0}\leq C_{A}^{-3}
, we get

\displaystyle\|\rho+A(U)\|_{a}\leq\|\rho\|_{a}+C_{A}\varepsilon_{0}^{\frac{2}{3}}\|U\|_{a}\leq\|\rho\|_{a}+\varepsilon_{0}^{\frac{1}{3}}\|U\|_{a}=\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

(4.7)

Thanks to Lemma
4.2
,

\|E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\leq c_{0}(a)\|\rho(\tau)+A(U)(\tau)\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\leq c_{0}(a)\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d+a}},

(4.8)

and there exists a constant
c_{1}>0
such that

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d-i+a}}d\tau\leq\frac{c_{1}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-i+a-1}},~~i=0,1,2,

(4.9)

where
c_{1}
only depends on
d
.

(1)
By (
4.8
) one has

\displaystyle\|Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|E(\tau,X_{\tau,t}(x,v))\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}d\tau\leq\int_{s}^{t}\frac{c_{0}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d-1+a}}d\tau\leq c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}},

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq

\displaystyle(1+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2+a}\|\nabla_{x}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}(1+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2+a}\|\nabla_{x}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d-1+a}}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}c_{1}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}(1+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2+a}\|\nabla_{x}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}).

Then, the right hand side can be absorbed by the left hand side provided that
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\frac{1}{400c_{0}c_{1}d^{2}}
.

By the same method, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}|\tau-s|\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(|\tau|+\|\nabla_{v}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}c_{1}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{d-3+a}}+\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-3+a}\|\nabla_{v}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right).

Hence,

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}\left(\|Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-3+a}\|\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 2c_{0}c_{1}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}},

(4.10)

provided
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\frac{1}{400c_{0}c_{1}d^{2}}.

(2)
By (
4.8
), (
4.10
), one obtains

\displaystyle\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\leq

\displaystyle\sup_{\alpha}\frac{1}{|\alpha|^{a}}\left[\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}d\tau\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\left(|\alpha|+\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)^{a}\left(1+\|\nabla_{x}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)d\tau\right]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}d\tau\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2+a}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}

\displaystyle+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\left(1+\|\nabla_{x}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)^{1+a}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2+a}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}+c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}.

Similarly,

\begin{split}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a-\delta}}&\leq\sup_{\alpha}\frac{1}{|\alpha|^{a-\delta}}\left(\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}d\tau\right.\\
&\left.+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{\dot{B}^{a-\delta}_{\infty,\infty}}\left(|\alpha\|\tau|+\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)^{a-\delta}\left(|\tau|+\|\nabla_{v}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)d\tau\right)\\
&\leq c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d+a-2}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-3+\delta}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a-\delta}}+c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\delta\langle s\rangle^{d-3+\delta}}.\end{split}

(4.11)

These imply

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\left(\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}+\delta\langle s\rangle^{d-3+\delta}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a-\delta}}\right)\lesssim_{a}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}},

(4.12)

provided
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\frac{1}{400c_{0}c_{1}d^{2}}
.

Combining this with (
4.10
), one gets (
4.4
). By the same argument, we also obtain (
4.5
). Thus we choose
\widetilde{\varepsilon_{0}}=\min\{C_{A}^{-3},\frac{1}{400c_{0}c_{1}d^{2}}\}
to finish the proof.

Remark 4.3

For
d\geq 4
, using the same method in (
4.11
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-3}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\lesssim_{a}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

(4.13)

Proposition 4.4

Let
a\in(\frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2},1)
,
\widetilde{\varepsilon_{0}}
be as in Proposition
4.1
. Assume that
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\leq\widetilde{\varepsilon_{0}}
and
\|(\rho,U)\|_{\gamma,b}\leq\mathbf{c}
for some
\gamma>0
and
b\in(0,1)
. Then, for
j=1,...,\gamma
and
\delta\in(0,b)
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-2+b}\|\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\langle s\rangle^{d-1+b}\|\nabla_{v}W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b}1,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{\gamma}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-2}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{j}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}+\langle s\rangle^{d-1}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{j}W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b}1,

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\delta\langle s\rangle^{d-3+\delta}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{\gamma+1}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b-\delta}}+\langle s\rangle^{d-2}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{a\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{\gamma+1}W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b}1,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\gamma+1}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-1+b}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}^{k}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}+\langle s\rangle^{d+b}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}^{k}W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b}1,

provided that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=2}^{\gamma+1}\sup_{|r|\leq 1}\left|{A^{(j)}(r)}\right|\leq C_{A,\gamma}.

(4.14)

Proof.
Note that for
0\leq j\leq\gamma
and
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla^{j}(A(U))\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}\leq

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{j}|A^{(k)}(U)|_{L^{\infty}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1},...,m_{k}\geq 1\\
m_{1}+..+m_{k}=j\end{subarray}}\left\|\prod_{i=1}^{k}\nabla^{m_{i}}U\right\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}+\sum_{k=1}^{j}|A^{(k)}(U)|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1},...,m_{k}\geq 1\\
m_{1}+..+m_{k}=j\end{subarray}}\left\|\prod_{i=1}^{k}\nabla^{m_{i}}U\right\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C_{A,\gamma}\left(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1},...,m_{k}\geq 1\\
m_{1}+..+m_{k}=j\end{subarray}}\left\|\prod_{i=1}^{k}\nabla^{m_{i}}U\right\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1},...,m_{k}\geq 1\\
m_{1}+..+m_{k}=j\end{subarray}}\left\|\prod_{i=1}^{k}\nabla^{m_{i}}U\right\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},C_{A,\gamma}}1.

Combining this with Lemma
4.2
, there exists a constant
c_{3}=c_{3}(d,b,b^{\prime},C_{A,\gamma},\mathbf{c})
such that

\sup_{\tau\geq 0}\sum_{j=0}^{\gamma+1}\langle\tau\rangle^{d+\min\{j+1,\gamma+b\}}\|\nabla_{x}^{j}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\langle\tau\rangle^{d+\min\{j+b^{\prime}+1,\gamma+b\}}\|\nabla_{x}^{j}E(\tau)\|_{\dot{B}^{b^{\prime}}_{\infty,\infty}}\lesssim_{b,b^{\prime}}\|\rho+A(U)\|_{\gamma,b}\leq c_{3},

(4.15)

for any
b^{\prime}\in(0,b]
.

(1)
Let
c_{1}
be in (
4.9
). As the proof of (
4.10
), we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq&\int_{s}^{t}|\tau-s|\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}|\tau|d\tau+\int_{s}^{t}|\tau-s|\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\||\nabla_{v}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}d\tau\\
\leq&c_{3}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{|\tau|^{2}}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d+b+1}}d\tau+c_{0}\left(\sup_{s\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2+b}\|\nabla_{v}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\int_{s}^{t}\frac{|\tau|\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2+b}\langle\tau\rangle^{d+a}}d\tau\\
\leq&c_{3}c_{1}\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{d+b-2}}+c_{0}c_{1}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2+b}\|\nabla_{v}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{d+b-2}}.\end{split}

(4.16)

Since
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\frac{1}{400_{0}c_{1}d^{2}}
, then we have

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-2+b}\|\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 2c_{3}c_{1}\mathbf{c}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,\gamma}}1.

Similarily, we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-1+b}\|\nabla_{v}W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,\gamma}}1.

(2)
As (
4.16
), it is easy to check that

\displaystyle\sum_{j=0}^{\gamma+1}\|\nabla_{v}^{j}\left(Y_{s,t},W_{s,t}\right)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sum_{j=0}^{\gamma}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{j}\left(Y_{s,t},W_{s,t}\right)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,\gamma},t}1,

(4.17)

for any
0\leq s\leq t<\infty
.

By (
4.17
), one has

\displaystyle\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{k+1}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b-\delta}}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{k+1}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b-\delta}}d\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla E(\tau)\|_{B^{b-\delta}_{\infty,\infty}}\left(|\tau|+\|\nabla_{v}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)^{b-\delta}\|\nabla_{v}^{k+1}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}d\tau

\displaystyle+C(\mathbf{c})\sum_{j=1}^{k}\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla^{j+1}E(\tau)\|_{B^{b-\delta}_{\infty,\infty}}(|\tau|+1)^{j+1+b-\delta}d\tau

\displaystyle+C(\mathbf{c})\sum_{j=1}^{k}\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla^{j+1}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}(|\tau|+1)^{j+1}d\tau

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{E estimate by rho},\eqref{E estimate by rho'}}{\leq}

\displaystyle c_{0}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d-1+a}}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{k+1}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b-\delta}}d\tau+\frac{C(\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,\gamma})}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+\delta}}+\frac{C(\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,\gamma})\textbf{1}_{k=\gamma}}{\delta\langle s\rangle^{d-3+\delta}}.

One derives that

\displaystyle\omega(s)\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{k+1}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b-\delta}}\leq\frac{1}{4}B(t)+C(\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,\gamma}),

where

B(t)=\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\omega(\tau)\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{k+1}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b-\delta}},~~~\omega(s)=\begin{cases}\langle s\rangle^{d-2}~~~\text{if}~~k\leq\gamma-1,\\
\delta\langle s\rangle^{d-3+\delta}~~\text{if}~~k=\gamma.\end{cases}

This implies

B(t)\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,\gamma}}1.

In particular,

\sum_{k=0}^{\gamma-1}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{k+1}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}+\delta\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-3+\delta}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{\gamma+1}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b-\delta}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,\gamma}}1.

(4.18)

Similarly, one also has

\displaystyle\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}^{k+1}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}^{k+1}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}d\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla E(\tau)\|_{B^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\|\nabla_{x}^{k+1}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}d\tau

\displaystyle+C(\mathbf{c})\sum_{j=1}^{k}\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\left(\|\nabla^{j+1}E(\tau)\|_{B^{b}_{\infty,\infty}}+\|\nabla^{j+1}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\right)d\tau

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{E estimate by rho},\eqref{E estimate by rho'}}{\leq}

\displaystyle c_{0}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d-1+a}}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}^{k+1}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}+\frac{C(\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,\gamma})}{\langle s\rangle^{d-1+b}}.

This gives

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-1+b}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}^{k+1}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,\gamma}}1.

(4.19)

By the same argument, we obtain

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-1-\mathbf{1}_{k=\gamma}}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{k+1}W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d+a}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}^{k+1}W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,\gamma}}1.

Therefore, the proof is complete.

Remark 4.5

For
d\geq 4
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-3}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{\gamma+1}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,\gamma}}1.

Proposition 4.6

Let
a\in(\frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2},1)
,
\widetilde{\varepsilon_{0}}
be as in Proposition
4.1
,
\rho_{1},\rho_{2}
be such that
\|(\rho_{1},U_{1})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}},\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\leq\widetilde{\varepsilon_{0}}
. Let
(X_{s,t}^{1},V_{s,t}^{1})
,
(X_{s,t}^{2},V_{s,t}^{2})
be solutions to (
2.1
) associated to
E^{1}(s,x)=\nabla_{x}(1-\Delta)^{-1}(\rho_{1}+A(U_{1}))(x)
and
E^{2}(s,x)=\nabla_{x}(1-\Delta)^{-1}(\rho_{2}+A(U_{2}))(x)
. Let
(Y_{s,t}^{1},W_{s,t}^{1})
,
(Y_{s,t}^{2},W_{s,t}^{2})
be such that

X_{s,t}^{i}(x,v)=x-(t-s)v+Y_{s,t}^{i}(x-vt,v),V_{s,t}^{i}(x,v)=v+W_{s,t}^{i}(x-vt,v),~~i=1,2.

(4.20)

Then for
\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}=Y_{s,t}^{1}-Y_{s,t}^{2},\mathbf{W}_{s,t}=W_{s,t}^{1}-W_{s,t}^{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}\left(\|\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\right)+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-2}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\lesssim_{a}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}},

(4.21)

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-1+a}\left(\|\mathbf{W}_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\mathbf{W}_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\right)+\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-1}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\mathbf{W}_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\lesssim_{a}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

(4.22)

Proof.
Let
c_{0},c_{1}
be the constant mentioned in the proof of Theorem
4.1
. We have

\displaystyle Y_{s,t}^{k}(x,v)=\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)E^{k}(\tau,x+\tau v+Y_{\tau,t}^{k}(x,v))d\tau,

\displaystyle W_{s,t}^{k}(x,v)=-\int_{s}^{t}E^{k}(\tau,x+\tau v+Y_{\tau,t}^{k}(x,v))d\tau,~~~k=1,2.

From
\|(\rho_{k},U_{k})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}
, one has
\|U_{k}\|_{a}\leq\varepsilon_{0}^{\frac{2}{3}}
, for
k=1,2
. Combining this with Assumption
3
, we obtain that
