\displaystyle\|A(U_{1})-A(U_{2})\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}=\left\|(U_{1}-U_{2})\int_{0}^{1}A^{\prime}(\varpi U_{1}+(1-\varpi U_{2}))d\varpi\right\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\|U_{1}-U_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\left\|\int_{0}^{1}A^{\prime}(\varpi U_{1}+(1-\varpi U_{2}))d\varpi\right\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}

\displaystyle+\|U_{1}-U_{2}\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\left\|\int_{0}^{1}A^{\prime}(\varpi U_{1}+(1-\varpi U_{2}))d\varpi\right\|_{L^{\infty}}

\displaystyle\leq\|U_{1}-U_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(\max_{|r|\leq\varepsilon_{0}^{2/3}}|A^{\prime}(r)|\right)^{1-a}\left(\varepsilon_{0}^{\frac{2}{3}}\max_{|r|\leq\varepsilon_{0}^{2/3}}|A^{\prime\prime}(r)|\right)^{a}+\|U_{1}-U_{2}\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\max_{|r|\leq\varepsilon_{0}^{2/3}}|A^{\prime}(r)|

\displaystyle\leq C_{A}\varepsilon_{0}^{\frac{2}{3}}\left(\|U_{1}-U_{2}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|U_{1}-U_{2}\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\right).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|(E^{1}-E^{2})(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla_{x}(E^{1}-E^{2})(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\nabla_{x}(E^{1}-E^{2})(\tau)\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\\
&\leq c_{0}(a)\|\rho_{1}(\tau)-\rho_{2}(\tau)+A(U_{1})(\tau)-A(U_{2})(\tau)\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\\
&\leq c_{0}(a)\left(\|\rho_{1}(\tau)-\rho_{2}(\tau)\|_{a}+C_{A}\varepsilon_{0}^{\frac{2}{3}}\left(\|U_{1}(\tau)-U_{2}(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|U_{1}(\tau)-U_{2}(\tau)\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\right)\right)\\
&\leq c_{0}(a)\frac{\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d+a}}.\end{split}

(4.23)

These imply

\displaystyle\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}(x,v)=

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)(E^{1}-E^{2})(\tau,x+\tau v+Y_{\tau,t}^{1}(x,v))d\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\left(E^{2}(\tau,x+\tau v+Y_{\tau,t}^{1}(x,v))-E^{2}(\tau,x+\tau v+Y_{\tau,t}^{2}(x,v))\right)d\tau.

Hence we know that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|(E^{1}-E^{2})(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}d\tau+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla E^{2}(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\|\mathbf{Y}_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq c_{0}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d-1+a}}d\tau+c_{0}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d-1+a}}\|\mathbf{Y}_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}+c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{2d-4+2a}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2+a}\|\mathbf{Y}_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}.

Since
\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\frac{1}{400_{0}c_{1}d^{2}}
,

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}\|\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 2c_{0}c_{1}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}\lesssim_{a}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}.

For the next terms, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|(E^{1}-E^{2})(\tau)\|_{B^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}d\tau+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla E^{2}(\tau)\|_{B^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\|\mathbf{Y}_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}d\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla E^{2}(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\mathbf{Y}_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}+2c_{0}^{2}c_{1}^{2}\frac{\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}

\displaystyle+c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{2(d-2+a)}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2+a}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\mathbf{Y}_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}+c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{2(d-2+a)}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2+a}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\mathbf{Y}_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}},

and

\displaystyle\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\leq

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|(E^{1}-E^{2})(\tau)\|_{B^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\langle\tau\rangle^{a}d\tau+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla E^{2}(\tau)\|_{B^{a}_{\infty,\infty}}\|\mathbf{Y}_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\langle\tau\rangle^{a}d\tau

\displaystyle+\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla E^{2}(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\mathbf{Y}_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2}}+2c_{0}^{2}c_{1}^{2}\frac{\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2}}

\displaystyle+c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{2(d-2)+a}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\mathbf{Y}_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2}}+c_{0}c_{1}\frac{\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{2(d-2)+a}}\sup_{0\leq\tau\leq t}\langle\tau\rangle^{d-2}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\mathbf{Y}_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}.

Therefore, we get (
4.21
) provided
\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\frac{1}{400_{0}c_{1}d^{2}}
. Similarly, we also get (
4.22
). The proof is complete.

Proposition 4.7

Let
a\in(\frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2},1)
,
\widetilde{\varepsilon_{0}}
be as in Proposition
4.1
. Let
(\rho,U)
be such that
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\leq\widetilde{\varepsilon_{0}}
. Then, for any
0\leq s\leq t<\infty
, we have a
C^{1}
map

(x,v)\mapsto\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)
satisfying for all
x,v\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
:

X_{s,t}(x,\Psi_{s,t}(x,v))=x-(t-s)v,\qquad

(4.24)

and

\langle s\rangle^{d-1+a}\left(\left|\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)-v\right|+\left|\nabla_{x}\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)\right|\right)+\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}\left|\nabla_{v}\left(\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)-v\right)\right|\lesssim_{a}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

(4.25)

Proof.
Define

\displaystyle\Phi_{s,t}(x,v)=-\frac{1}{t-s}\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)E(\tau,x-(t-\tau)v+Y_{\tau,t}(x-vt,v))d\tau.

Hence, it follows from (
4.1
) that

\displaystyle X_{s,t}(x,v)

\displaystyle=x-v(t-s)+Y_{s,t}(x-vt,v)=x-(t-s)(v+\Phi_{s,t}(x,v)).

(4.26)

For the first estimate, since
\|\nabla_{x}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim 1
, so

\|\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim\int_{s}^{t}\|E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\lesssim_{a}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d+a-1}}

(4.27)

and

\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq\frac{1}{t-s}\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}(1+\|\nabla_{x}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}})d\tau\lesssim_{a}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d+a-1}},

(4.28)

we have

\sup_{0\leq s,t\leq\infty}\langle s\rangle^{d+a-1}\left(\|\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\lesssim_{a}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

(4.29)

Now one has

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{v}\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t-s}\int_{s}^{t}(\tau-s)\|\nabla_{x}E(\tau)\|_{L^{\infty}}\left(t-\tau+t\|\nabla_{x}Y_{\tau,t}||_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{v}Y_{\tau,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\frac{c_{0}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t-s}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{\tau-s}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d+a}}\left((t-\tau)+(t-\tau)+\tau+1\right)d\tau

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 4c_{0}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{s}^{t}\frac{1}{\langle\tau\rangle^{d+a-1}}d\tau.

Let
c_{0}
,
c_{1}
be in (
4.8
) and (
4.9
) by (
4.4
) and (
4.8
). One obtains that

\|\nabla_{v}\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq\frac{4c_{0}c_{1}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}\leq\frac{1}{100d^{2}},

provided
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\frac{1}{400c_{0}c_{1}d^{2}}
,
and it is clear that under the assumption
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\frac{1}{400c_{0}c_{1}d^{2}}
, the map
(x,v)\mapsto(x,v+\Phi_{s,t}(x,v))
is a
C^{1}
differomorphism. Thus, there exists a map
(x,v)\mapsto\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)
satisfying (
4.24
). Moreover, combining (
4.24
) with (
4.26
), we have

\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)+\Phi_{s,t}(x,\Psi_{s,t}(x,v))=v.

Now we have

\displaystyle\left|\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)-v\right|\leq\|\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim_{a}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d+a-1}},

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{x}\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)\right|\leq\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{v}\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}|\nabla_{x}\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)|,

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{v}\left(\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)-v\right)\right|\leq\|\nabla_{v}\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{v}\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\left(|\nabla_{v}(\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)-v)|+1\right).

Then, the right hand side can be absorbed by the left. Hence, we get

\displaystyle\left\|\nabla_{x}\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 2\|\nabla_{x}\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim_{a}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d+a-1}},

\displaystyle\left|\nabla_{v}\left(\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)-v\right)\right|\leq 3\|\nabla_{v}\Phi_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim_{a}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}.

These follow (
4.25
).

Remark 4.8

For
a_{1}\in(0,1)
, if we also have
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a_{1}}}\lesssim 1
,

\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a_{1}}\left|\nabla_{v}\left(\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)-v\right)\right|\lesssim_{a_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a_{1}}}.

(4.30)

5
Contribution of the initial data

For
h=h(x,v):\mathbb{R}^{d}_{x}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}_{v}\to\mathbb{R}
, define

\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t,x,v)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}h(X_{0,t}(x,v),V_{0,t}(x,v))dv.

(5.1)

Proposition 5.1

Let
a\in(\frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2},1)
,
\widetilde{\varepsilon_{0}}
be as in Proposition
4.1
. Let
(\rho,U)
be such that
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\leq\widetilde{\varepsilon_{0}}
. Then there holds

\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)\|_{\sigma}\lesssim_{a}\sum_{p=1,\infty}\|\mathcal{D}^{\sigma}(h)\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}},

(5.2)

for any
\sigma\in[a,1)
.
Moreover, for
0<\delta_{0}<2-\sqrt{3}
, if
\|(\rho,U)\|_{l,1-\delta_{0}}\leq\mathbf{c}
for some
l\geq 0
, then for any
b\in(0,1-\delta_{0})
, we have

\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)\|_{l+1,b}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},a,b,C_{A,l}}\frac{1}{1-\delta_{0}-b}\sum_{i=1}^{l+1}\|\mathcal{D}^{b}(\nabla^{i}_{x,v}h)\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}},

(5.3)

provided that
\sum_{j=2}^{l+1}\sup_{|r|\leq 1}\left|{A^{(j)}(r)}\right|\leq C_{A,l}.

Remark 5.2

By (
4.13
), for
d\geq 4
, we can obtain better result as follows

\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)\|_{l+1,1-\delta_{0}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},a,b,C_{A,l}}\sum_{i=1}^{l+1}\|\mathcal{D}^{1-\delta_{0}}(\nabla^{i}_{x,v}h)\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}}.

(5.4)

Remark 5.3

We can not only use
\|h\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{1}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}
to control
\|I_{h}(\rho,U)(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}
for
0\leq t\leq 1
.

For example we take

h(x,v)=\frac{1}{|x|^{\theta}(|x|^{2}+|v|^{2}+1)^{2d}},~~X_{0,t(x,v)}=x-tv,~~V_{0,t}(x,v)=v,~~U=0,

for
0<\theta<d
. And it is easy to check that for
t\in[0,1]
, we have

\|h\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{1}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}<\infty,~~\|h\|_{L^{1}_{v}L^{\infty}_{x}}=\infty,~\text{and}~~\|I_{h}(\rho,U)(t)\|_{L^{\infty}}\sim\frac{1}{t^{\theta}}.

Proof.

(1)
Firstly, we deal with
\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t,x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}
. Note that

(x,v)\mapsto(X_{0,t}(x,v),V_{0,t}(x,v))
corresponding to the transport matrix

\displaystyle\mbox{$\mathfrak{A}(t,x,v):=$}\left[\begin{array}[]{cc}I_{d}+\nabla_{x}Y_{0,t}(x-vt,v)&-tI_{d}+\nabla_{v}Y_{0,t}(x-vt,v)\\
\nabla_{x}W_{0,t}(x-vt,v)&I_{d}+\nabla_{v}W_{0,t}(x-vt,v)\end{array}\right].

Since we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x,v}Y_{0,t}(x-vt,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x,v}W_{0,t}(x-vt,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 2c_{0}c_{1}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq 2c_{0}c_{1}\varepsilon_{0}\leq\frac{1}{400d^{2}},

and
\|\det\mathfrak{A}^{-1}(t,x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim 1
. Then
we directly obtain that

\begin{split}\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t,x)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}=&\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}h(x-vt+Y_{0,t}(x-vt,v),v+W_{0,t}(x-vt,v))dv\right\|_{L^{1}_{x}}\\
=&\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}h(x,v)|\det\mathfrak{A}^{-1}(t,x,v)|dv\right\|_{L^{1}_{x}}\lesssim\|h\|_{L^{1}_{v}L^{1}_{x}}.\end{split}

(5.5)

For the
\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t,x)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}
with
t\leq 1
, changing variable as
\tilde{v}=v+W_{0,t}(x-vt,v)
, we have

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t,x)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}=&\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}h(X_{0,t}(x,v),\tilde{v})\det{(Id+\nabla_{v}W_{0,t}(x-vt,v))^{-1}}{d\tilde{v}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}h(X_{0,t}(x,v),\tilde{v})d\tilde{v}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim_{a}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\|h(\cdot,\tilde{v})\|_{L^{\infty}}d\tilde{v}=\|h\|_{L^{1}_{v}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(5.6)

For
t\geq 1
, we change of variable
w=x-vt
to get more decay in time:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t,x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}h\left(w+Y_{0,t}\left(w,\frac{x-w}{t}\right),\frac{x-w}{t}+W_{0,t}\left(w,\frac{x-w}{t}\right)\right)\frac{dw}{t^{d}}.

Thus

\begin{split}\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t,x)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}=&\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}h(w,V_{0,t}(x,\Psi_{0,t}(x,\frac{x-w}{t})))\det((\nabla_{v}\Psi_{0,t})(x,\frac{x-w}{t}))\frac{dw}{t^{d}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\lesssim&\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}h\left(w,V_{0,t}\left(x,\Psi_{0,t}(x,\frac{x-w}{t})\right)\right)\frac{dw}{t^{d}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\left\|\det\left((\nabla_{v}\Psi_{0,t})(x,\frac{x-w}{t})\right)\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,w}}\\
\lesssim_{a}&\frac{1}{t^{d}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\|h(w,.)\|_{L^{\infty}}dw=\frac{1}{t^{d}}\|h\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{\infty}_{v}}.\end{split}

(5.7)

Combine (
5.5
) with (
5.6
) and (
5.7
) , we have

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t)\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{a}\|h\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}},~~p=1,\infty.

(5.8)

(2)
Now we deal with the term
\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t)\|_{B^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}
. From Proposition
4.1
, we have

\displaystyle\|\nabla_{x}X(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\nabla_{x}V(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim_{a}1,

\displaystyle\sup_{\alpha/t}\frac{\left|\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}Y_{0,t}\left(w,\cdot\right)\left(\frac{x-w}{t}\right)\right|}{|\alpha/t|}+\sup_{\alpha/t}\frac{\left|\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}W_{0,t}\left(w,\cdot\right)\left(\frac{x-w}{t}\right)\right|}{|\alpha/t|}\lesssim_{a}1.

Thus, we get

\displaystyle\frac{|\delta_{\alpha}\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t,x)|}{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}\lesssim_{a}\sum_{z=x,x-\alpha}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{\sigma}(h)(X_{0,t}(z,v),V_{0,t}(z,v))dv,

and

\displaystyle\frac{|\delta_{\alpha}\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t,x)|}{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}\lesssim_{a}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{\sigma}_{1}(h)\left(w+Y_{0,t}\left(w,\frac{x-w}{t}\right),\frac{x-w}{t}+W_{0,t}\left(w,\frac{x-w}{t}\right)\right)\frac{dw}{t^{d+\sigma}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{\sigma}_{2}(h)\left(w+Y_{0,t}\left(w,\frac{x-\alpha-w}{t}\right),\frac{x-\alpha-w}{t}+W_{0,t}\left(w,\frac{x-\alpha-w}{t}\right)\right)\frac{dw}{t^{d+\sigma}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\lesssim_{a}\frac{1}{t^{\sigma}}\sum_{z=v,v-\frac{\alpha}{t}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{\sigma}(h)(X_{0,t}(x,z),V_{0,t}(x,z))dv.

Thanks to (
5.8
) with
h=\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{\sigma}(h)
, we get

\langle t\rangle^{\sigma+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\lesssim_{a}\|\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{\sigma}(h)\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}},~~p=1,\infty.

(5.9)

Until now we finish the proof of (
5.2
).

(3)
Finally we consider the term
\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)\|_{l+1,b}
. Thanks to Proposition
4.4
, one has

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{l+1}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}\nabla_{x}^{k}(Y,W)_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}+\sum_{k=1}^{l+1}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\nabla_{x}^{k}(Y,W)_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,l}}1.

Therefore

\displaystyle\frac{|\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{l}\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t,x)|}{|\alpha|^{b}}\lesssim

{}_{\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,l}}\sum_{j=1}^{l}\sum_{z=x,x-\alpha}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathcal{D}^{b}(\nabla^{j}_{x,v}h)\left(X_{0,t}(z,v),V_{0,t}(z,v)\right){dw}.

Thus,

\|\nabla^{l}\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t)\|_{\dot{B}^{b}_{p,\infty}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},a,b,C_{A,l}}\sum_{j=1}^{l}\|\mathcal{D}^{b}(\nabla^{j}_{x,v}h)\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}}.

(5.10)

On the other hand, changing variable as
w=x-vt
and using the estimation

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{l+1}(1-\delta_{0}-b)\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}^{v}\nabla_{v}^{k}(Y,W)_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}+\sum_{k=1}^{l+1}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|\nabla_{v}^{k}(Y,W)_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b,C_{A,l}}1,

we have

\displaystyle\frac{|\delta_{\alpha}\nabla^{l}\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t,x)|}{|\alpha|^{b}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b}\frac{1}{1-\delta_{0}-b}\frac{1}{t^{d+l+b}}\Bigg[\sum_{j=1}^{l}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathcal{D}^{b}_{1}(\nabla^{j}_{x,v}h)\left(w+Y_{0,t}\left(w,\frac{x-w}{t}\right),\frac{x-w}{t}+W_{0,t}\left(w,\frac{x-w}{t}\right)\right)dw

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathcal{D}^{b}_{2}(\nabla^{j}_{x,v}h)\left(w+Y_{0,t}\left(w,\frac{x-\alpha-w}{t}\right),\frac{x-\alpha-w}{t}+W_{0,t}\left(w,\frac{x-\alpha-w}{t}\right)\right)dw\Bigg]

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},b}\frac{1}{(1-\delta_{0}-b)t^{l+b}}\sum_{j=1}^{l}\sum_{z=v,v-\alpha/t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(\mathcal{D}^{b}(\nabla^{j}_{x,v}h)\right)(X_{0,t}(x,z),V_{0,t}(x,z))dv.

Thanks to (
5.8
), we get for
p=1,\infty

t^{l+b-\delta+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|\nabla^{l}\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho,U)(t)\|_{B^{b-\delta}_{p,\infty}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c},a,b,C_{A,l}}\frac{1}{1-\delta_{0}-b}\sum_{j=1}^{l}\|\mathcal{D}^{b^{\prime}}(\nabla^{j}_{x,v}h)\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}}.

(5.11)

Therefore, we obtain (
5.3
).

Proposition 5.4

Let
a\in(\frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2},1)
,
\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{0}
be as in Proposition
4.1
. Suppose that
\|(\rho_{1},U_{1})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}},\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\leq\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{0}
. There holds,

\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{a}\lesssim_{a}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}\sum_{p=1,\infty}\|\mathcal{D}^{a}(\nabla_{x,v}h)\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}}.

(5.12)

Proof.
Let
X_{s,t}^{1}
,
X_{s,t}^{2}
,
Y_{s,t}^{1}
,
Y_{s,t}^{2}
,
V_{s,t}^{1}
,
V_{s,t}^{2}
,
\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}
,
\mathbf{W}_{s,t}
be in Proposition
4.6
. Then we have

\displaystyle X_{s,t}^{1}(x,v)-X_{s,t}^{2}(x,v)=Y_{s,t}^{1}(x-vt,v)-Y_{s,t}^{2}(x-vt,v)=\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}(x-vt,v),

\displaystyle V_{s,t}^{1}(x,v)-V_{s,t}^{2}(x,v)=W_{s,t}^{1}(x-vt,v)-W_{s,t}^{2}(x-vt,v)=\mathbf{W}_{s,t}(x-vt,v).

Meanwhile, denote

\displaystyle\begin{split}&X_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi)=(1-\varpi)X_{s,t}^{1}(x,v)+\varpi X_{s,t}^{2}(x,v),~~V_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi)=(1-\varpi)V_{s,t}^{1}(x,v)+\varpi V_{s,t}^{2}(x,v),\\
&Y_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi)=(1-\varpi)Y_{s,t}^{1}(x,v)+\varpi Y_{s,t}^{2}(x,v),~~W_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi)=(1-\varpi)W_{s,t}^{1}(x,v)+\varpi W_{s,t}^{2}(x,v).\end{split}

(5.13)

(1)
For the term
\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{1},U_{1})(t)-\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{2},U_{2})(t)\|_{L^{p}}
, we have

\displaystyle|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{1},U_{1})(t,x)-\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{2},U_{2})(t,x)|\leq\left(\|\mathbf{Y}_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\mathbf{W}_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{0}^{1}\left|(\nabla_{x,v}h)(X_{0,t}(x,v,\varpi),V_{0,t}(x,v,\varpi))\right|d\varpi dv

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{results of W12 small}\eqref{results of Y12 small}}{\lesssim_{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{0\leq\varpi\leq 1}\left|(\nabla_{x,v}h)(X_{0,t}(x,v,\varpi),V_{0,t}(x,v,\varpi))\right|dv.

As the proof of (
5.8
), one obtains

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{0\leq\varpi\leq 1}\left|(\nabla_{x,v}h)(X_{0,t}(x,v,\varpi),V_{0,t}(x,v,\varpi))\right|dv\lesssim_{a}\sum_{p=1,\infty}\|\nabla_{x,v}h\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}}.

Thus

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1,\infty}\langle t\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{1})(t)-\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{2})(t)\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{a}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}\sum_{p=1,\infty}\|\nabla_{x,v}h\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}}.

(5.14)

(2)
Now we consider the term
\|\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{1},U_{1})(t)-\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{2},U_{2})(t)\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}
. We compute directly to get

\begin{split}&\frac{|\delta_{\alpha}\left(\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\right)(t,x)|}{|\alpha|^{a}}\\
&\quad\leq\left(\sup_{z}\frac{\|\delta_{z}^{x}\mathbf{Y}_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|z|^{a}}+\sup_{z}\frac{\|\delta_{z}^{x}\mathbf{W}_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|z|^{a}}\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{0}^{1}\left|(\nabla_{x,v}h)(X_{0,t}(x,v,\varpi),V_{0,t}(x,v,\varpi))\right|d\varpi dv\\
&\quad\quad+\left(\|\mathbf{Y}_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\mathbf{W}_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{0}^{1}\sum_{z=x,x-\alpha}\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{a}(\nabla_{x,v}h)(X_{0,t}(z,v,\varpi),V_{0,t}(z,v,\varpi))d\varpi dv\\
&\overset{\eqref{results of W12 small}\eqref{results of Y12 small}}{\lesssim_{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}\sum_{z=x,x-\alpha}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{0\leq\varpi\leq 1}\mathcal{D}^{a}(\nabla_{x,v}h)(X_{0,t}(z,v,\varpi),V_{0,t}(z,v,\varpi))d\varpi dv.\end{split}

(5.15)

On the other hand, we first make the change of variable as
w=x-vt
, then taking
\delta_{\alpha}
to get the decay of
t^{-a}
, after this we go back to the original variable to obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\frac{|\delta_{\alpha}\left(\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\right)(t,x)|}{|\alpha|^{a}}\\
&\quad\leq\frac{1}{t^{a}}\left(\sup_{z}\frac{\|\delta_{z}^{v}\mathbf{Y}_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|z|^{a}}+\sup_{z}\frac{\|\delta_{z}^{v}\mathbf{W}_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}}{|z|^{a}}\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{0}^{1}\left|(\nabla_{x,v}h)(X_{0,t}(x,v,\varpi),V_{0,t}(x,v,\varpi))\right|d\varpi dv\\
&\qquad+\frac{1}{t^{a}}\left(\|\mathbf{Y}_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}+\|\mathbf{W}_{0,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{0}^{1}\sum_{z=x,x-\alpha}\dot{\mathcal{D}}^{a}(\nabla_{x,v}h)(X_{0,t}(z,v,\varpi),V_{0,t}(z,v,\varpi))d\varpi dv\\
&\overset{\eqref{results of W12 small}\eqref{results of Y12 small}}{\lesssim_{a}}\frac{\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}}{t^{a}}\sum_{z=x,x-\alpha}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{0\leq\varpi\leq 1}\mathcal{D}^{a}(\nabla_{x,v}h)(X_{0,t}(z,v,\varpi),V_{0,t}(z,v,\varpi))d\varpi dv.\end{split}

(5.16)

Combining (
5.15
) with (
5.16
), we have

\displaystyle\frac{|\delta_{\alpha}\left(\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\right)(t,x)|}{|\alpha|^{a}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim_{a}\frac{\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}}{\langle t\rangle^{a}}\sum_{z=x,x-\alpha}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{0\leq\varpi\leq 1}\mathcal{D}^{a}(\nabla_{x,v}h)(X_{0,t}(z,v,\varpi),V_{0,t}(z,v,\varpi))d\varpi dv.

Therefore, applying the the same method in (
5.8
), we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{t>0}\langle t\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|(\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{I}_{h}(\rho_{2},U_{2}))(t)\|_{\dot{B}^{a}_{p,\infty}}\lesssim_{a}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S_{a}^{\varepsilon_{0}}}\sum_{p=1,\infty}\|\mathcal{D}^{a}(\nabla_{x,v}h)\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}}.

(5.17)

Now the proof is complete.

6
Contribution Of The Reaction Term

In this section, we focus on estimate of the reaction term:

\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)(t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}E(s,x-(t-s)v)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mu(v)dvds-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}E(s,X_{s,t}(x,v))\cdot\nabla_{v}\mu(V_{s,t}(x,v))dvds.

(6.1)

Denote that for any given
F:\mathbb{R}^{+}\times\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
and
\eta:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t,x)=-\mathcal{T}_{NL}[F,\eta](t,x)+\mathcal{T}_{L}[F,\eta](t,x),

with

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{L}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F(s,x-(t-s)v)\eta(v)dvds,

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{NL}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F(s,X_{s,t}(x,v))\eta(V_{s,t}(x,v))dvds.

Hence, it is clear that

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}(\rho)=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathcal{T}[E_{i},\partial_{x_{i}}\mu].

By changing variable, we reformuate as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{L}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F(s,w+\frac{s}{t}(x-w))\eta(\frac{x-w}{t})\frac{dwds}{t^{d}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{NL}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F\left(s,Y_{s,t}(w,\frac{x-w}{t})+w+\frac{s(x-w)}{t}\right)\eta\left(W_{s,t}(w,\frac{x-w}{t})+\frac{x-w}{t}\right)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}}.

Then, we have following Lemmas.

Lemma 6.1

Let
a\in(\frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2},1)
and
\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{0}>0
be as in Proposition
4.1
. Assume that
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{0}
. Let
(X_{s,t},V_{s,t})
be solution to (
2.1
) associated to
E(s,x)=\nabla_{x}(1-\Delta)^{-1}(\rho(s,x))
. Let
Y_{s,t},W_{s,t}
be such that

X_{s,t}(x,v)=x-(t-s)v+Y_{s,t}(x-vt,v),V_{s,t}(x,v)=v+W_{s,t}(x-vt,v).

(6.2)

Suppose
\eta
be such that

\sum_{j=0}^{2}\langle v\rangle^{N}|\nabla^{j}\eta(v)|\leq\mathbf{c}_{1},

(6.3)

for
N>d
.
Then,

(1)

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t,x)\right|\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|F(s,x-(t-s)v)|\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}\frac{dvds}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}.

(6.4)

In particular, for
p=1,\infty

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{L^{1}}\right).

(6.5)

(2)

For any
\sigma\in[a,1)

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1,\infty}\langle t\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\sigma}\|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\sum_{p=1,\infty}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\sigma}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\right).

(6.6)

Proof.

(1)

First of all, we change of variable and obtain

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t,x)|=

\displaystyle\left|-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F(s,x-(t-s)v)\eta(V_{s,t}(x,\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)))|det(\nabla_{v}\Psi_{s,t}(x,v))|dvds\right.

\displaystyle\left.+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F(s,x-(t-s)v)\eta(v)dvds\right|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|F(s,x-(t-s)v)|\left|\eta(V_{s,t}(x,\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)))-\eta(v)\right|dvds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|F(s,x-(t-s)v)|\Big||\det(\nabla_{v}\Psi_{s,t}(x,v))|-1\Big|\left|\eta(V_{s,t}(x,\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)))\right|dvds.

Hence, since we have

\displaystyle\left|\eta(V_{s,t}(x,\Psi_{s,t}(x,v)))-\eta(v)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{1}\left|\nabla\eta(\varpi V_{s,t}(x,v)+(1-\varpi)v)\right|V_{s,t}(x,v)-v|d\varpi

\displaystyle{\lesssim_{\mathbf{c}_{1}}}\sup_{\varpi\in[0,1]}\frac{|V_{s,t}(x,v)-v|}{\langle\varpi W_{s,t}(x-vt,v)+v\rangle^{N}}

\displaystyle\sim_{\mathbf{c}_{1}}\frac{|W_{s,t}(x-vt,v)|}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}\overset{\eqref{results of W small},\eqref{z3}}{\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{{d-1+a}}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}},

and

\left|\eta(V_{s,t}(x,\Psi_{s,t}(t,x)))\right|{\lesssim_{\mathbf{c}_{1}}}{\langle W_{s,t}(x-t\Psi_{s,t}(t,x),\Psi_{s,t}(t,x))+v\rangle^{-N}}{\lesssim_{\mathbf{c}_{1}}}{\langle v\rangle^{-N}}
,
where we use the fact that

\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\|W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 1
in (
4.5
). Hence

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t,x)\right|

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{z3}}{\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|F(s,x-(t-s)v)|\left(\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-1+a}}+\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}\right)\frac{dvds}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|F(s,x-(t-s)v)|\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}\frac{dvds}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}.

This gives (
6.4
). Moreover,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{t}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|F(s,x-(t-s)v)|\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}.

Then, we apply Lemma
8.1
with
\mathcal{H}_{1}=F
and
\mathcal{H}_{2}=0
to obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}

\displaystyle t^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{t/2}\|F(s)\|_{L^{1}}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}+\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{t/2}^{t}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}

\displaystyle\underbrace{t^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{L^{1}}\int_{0}^{t/2}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}}_{\text{Thanks to }\eqref{mathcal H 1}}

\displaystyle+\underbrace{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}\right)\int_{t/2}^{t}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}}_{\text{Thanks to }\eqref{mathcal H p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{L^{1}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}\right).

Combining this with

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{t}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}ds\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}},

to obtain (
6.5
).

(2)
Now we estimate
\|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}.

2.1)
Estimate
\|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}
for
0\leq t<1
.

We divide
\delta_{\alpha}\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t,x)
into three terms:

\displaystyle\delta_{\alpha}\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t,x)=\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{1}[F,\eta](t,x)|+\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{2}[F,\eta](t,x)+\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{3}[F,\eta](t,x),

where

\displaystyle\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{1}[F,\eta](t,x)=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\delta_{\alpha}F(s,.)\left(X_{s,t}(x,v)\right)\eta\left(V_{s,t}(x,v)\right)dvds+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\delta_{\alpha}F(s,.)(x-(t-s)v)\eta(v)dvds,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}}^{2}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left[F\left(s,X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v)\right)-F\left(s,,X_{s,t}(x,v)-\alpha\right)\right]\eta\left(V_{s,t}(x,v)\right)dvds,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{3}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F\left(s,X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v)\right)\left[\eta\left(V_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v)\right)-\eta\left(V_{s,t}(x,v)\right)\right]dvds.

For the first term, using (
6.3
) and (
6.4
) we have

\displaystyle\|\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{1}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle=\|\mathcal{T}[\delta_{\alpha}F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|\delta_{\alpha}F(s)\|_{L^{1}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|\delta_{\alpha}F(s)\|_{L^{p}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}|\alpha|^{\sigma}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{1,\infty}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\right).

Since

\displaystyle\left|X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v)-\left(X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v)-\alpha\right)\right|=\left|Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v)-Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v)\right|\leq|\alpha|\|\nabla_{x}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}},

we can estimate
\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{2}[F,\eta](t,x)
as follows

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{2}[F,\eta](t,x)\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim_{\mathbf{c}_{1}}

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{\sigma}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\|\sup_{z}|z|^{-\sigma}|\delta_{z}F(s,.)\|_{L^{p}}\|\nabla_{x}Y_{{s,t}}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}^{\sigma}\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{\sigma}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{-(d+a-2)\sigma}ds\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}|\alpha|^{\sigma}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}},

where in the last inequality, we use
(d+a-3)\sigma+a\geq(a+1)a>1
.

Note that by (
4.5
), one has

\sup_{s,t}\|W_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq 1.

Then,

\displaystyle\langle\varpi V_{s,t}(x,v)+(1-\varpi)V_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v)\rangle=\langle v+\varpi W_{s,t}(x-tv,v)+(1-\varpi)W_{s,t}(x-\alpha-tv,v)\rangle\sim\langle v\rangle.

(6.7)

We can estimate
\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{3}[F,\eta](t)
.

\displaystyle\|\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{3}[F,\eta](t,x)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\int_{0}^{1}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}\left|\nabla\eta(\cdot)\right|(\varpi V_{s,t}(x,v)+(1-\varpi)V_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v))\left|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}V_{s,t}(x,v)\right|dvdsd\varpi

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbf{c}_{1}}|\alpha|^{\sigma}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\left|\delta_{\alpha}^{x}V_{s,t}(x,v)\right|}{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}dvds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}|\alpha|^{\sigma}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-1+a}}\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}|\alpha|^{\sigma}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}},

where we apply (
4.5
).

In conclusion,
for
0<t<1
, we get

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}

\displaystyle\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}\right).

(6.8)

2.2)
Estimate
\|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}
for
t\geq 1
.

Set

\displaystyle\begin{split}&Z_{1}(x)=Y_{s,t}(w,\frac{x-w}{t})+w+\frac{s(x-w)}{t},~Z_{2}(x)=W_{s,t}(w,\frac{x-w}{t})+\frac{x-w}{t},\\
&Z_{3}(x)=W_{s,t}(w,\frac{x-\alpha-w}{t})+\frac{x-w}{t},~~Z_{4}(x)=Y_{s,t}(w,\frac{x-w}{t})+w+\frac{s(x-\alpha-w)}{t}.\end{split}

(6.9)

We have

\displaystyle\delta_{\alpha}\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t,x)=\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{1}[F,\eta](t,x)|+\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{2}[F,\eta](t,x)+\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{3}[F,\eta](t,x)+\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{4}[F,\eta](t,x),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{1}[F,\eta](t,x)=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}F(s,.)\left(Z_{1}(x)\right)\eta\left(Z_{2}(x)\right)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}}+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}F(s,.)(w+\frac{s}{t}(x-w))\eta(\frac{x-w}{t})\frac{dwds}{t^{d}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{2}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F\left(s,Z_{1}(x-\alpha)\right)(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\eta)\left(Z_{2}(x)\right)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}}-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F(s,w+\frac{s}{t}(x-w-\alpha))(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\eta)(\frac{x-w}{t})\frac{dwds}{t^{d}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{3}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(F\left(s,Z_{1}(x-\alpha)\right)-F\left(s,Z_{4}(x)\right)\right)\eta\left(Z_{2}(x)\right)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{4}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F\left(s,Z_{1}(x-\alpha)\right)\left(\eta\left(Z_{3}(x)\right)-\eta\left(Z_{2}(x)\right)\right)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}}.

Going back to the variable
v=\frac{x-w}{t}
,
and denote

\displaystyle Z_{5}(x)=Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v-\frac{\alpha}{t})+x-(t-s)v-\frac{s\alpha}{t},~~Z_{6}(x)=W_{s,t}(x-tv,v-\frac{\alpha}{t})+v,

we reformulate as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{1}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(-\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}F(s,.)\left(X_{s,t}(x,v)\right)\eta\left(V_{s,t}(x,v)\right)+\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}F(s,.)(x-(t-s)v)\eta(v)\right)dvds,

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{2}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(F\left(s,X_{s,t}(x,v)\right)\left(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\eta\right)\left(V_{s,t}(x,v)\right)-F(s,x-(t-s)v)\left(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\eta\right)(v)\right)dvds,

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{3}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(F\left(s,X_{s,t}(x,v)-\frac{s\alpha}{t}\right)-F\left(s,Z_{5}(x)\right)\right)\eta\left(V_{s,t}(x,v)\right)dvds,

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{4}[F,\eta](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F\left(s,Z_{5}(x)\right)\left(\eta\left(V_{s,t}(x,v)\right)-\eta\left(Z_{6}(x)\right)\right)dvds.

Note that for
p=1,\infty
and
|\alpha|\geq t

\displaystyle\langle t\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}

\displaystyle\leq 2\langle t\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|\delta_{\alpha}\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}\leq 4\langle t\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{z21}}{\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{L^{1}}\right).

So, it is enough to consider
|\alpha|\leq t
.

2.2.1)
Estimate
\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{1}[F,\eta](t,x)
.

Applying (
6.5
) with
\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}F
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{1}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle=\|\mathcal{T}[\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}t^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}F(s)\|_{L^{1}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}F(s)\|_{L^{p}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}|\alpha|^{\sigma}t^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}-\sigma}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{1,\infty}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\right),

where we apply
\sigma<2a
.
Thus, we can yield

\displaystyle t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\sigma}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{1}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}}{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{1,\infty}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\right).

(6.10)

2.2.2)
Estimate
\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{2}[F,\eta](t,x)
.

Note that

\displaystyle|\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\eta(v)|+|\nabla_{v}(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\eta)(v)|\lesssim_{\mathbf{c}_{1}}\min\left\{\frac{|\alpha|}{t},1\right\}\left(\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}+\frac{1}{\langle v-\frac{\alpha}{t}\rangle^{N}}\right)\lesssim_{\mathbf{c}_{1}}\frac{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}{t^{\sigma}}\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}},

since
|\alpha|\leq t
.
Thus, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{2}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle=\frac{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}{t^{\sigma}}\left\|\mathcal{T}\left[F,\frac{t^{\sigma}}{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\eta(v)\right]\right\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\frac{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}{t^{\sigma+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{L^{1}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}\right),

which implies

\displaystyle t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\sigma}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{2}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}}{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{L^{1}}\right).

2.2.3)
Estimate
\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{3}[F,\eta](t,x)
.

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{3}[F,\eta](t,x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbf{c}_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left|F\left(s,X_{s,t}(x,v)-\frac{s\alpha}{t}\right)-F\left(s,Z_{5}(x)\right)\right|\frac{dvds}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\mathbf{c}_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(\sup_{z}|z|^{-\sigma}\left|\delta_{z}F(s,.)\left(Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v)+x-(t-s)v-\frac{s\alpha}{t}\right)\right|\right)\left|Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v)-Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v-\frac{\alpha}{t})\right|^{\sigma}\frac{dvds}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\frac{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}{t^{\sigma}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sup_{z}|z|^{-\sigma}\left|\delta_{z}F(s,.)\left(Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v)+x-(t-s)v-\frac{s\alpha}{t}\right)\right|\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{\sigma(d-3+a)}}\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}.

(6.11)

Thus applying Lemma
8.1
with
\mathcal{H}=\sup_{z}|z|^{-\sigma}|\delta_{z}F(s,.)|
and
\varphi(x,v)=Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v-\frac{\alpha}{t})-\frac{s\alpha}{t}
to obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{3}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\underbrace{\frac{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\sigma}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\int_{0}^{t/2}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{1,\infty}}\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{\sigma(d-3+a)}}ds}_{\text{Thanks to }\eqref{mathcal H 1}}+\underbrace{\frac{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}{t^{\sigma}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\int_{t/2}^{t}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{\sigma(d-3+a)}}ds}_{\text{Thanks to }\eqref{mathcal H p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\frac{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\sigma}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{1,\infty}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\right)\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{\sigma(d-3+a)+a}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\frac{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\sigma}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{1,\infty}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\right),

where in the last line, we use the fact that
a^{2}+a>1
.
Thus,

\displaystyle t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\sigma}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{3}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}}{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{1,\infty}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\right).

2.2.4)
Estimate
\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{4}[F,\eta](t,x)
.

One has

\displaystyle|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{4}[F,\eta](t,x)|\lesssim_{\mathbf{c}_{1}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left|F\left(s,Z_{5}(x)\right)\right|\left|W_{s,t}(x-tv,v)-W_{s,t}(x-tv,v-\frac{\alpha}{t})\right|\frac{dvds}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\min\left\{1,\frac{|\alpha|}{t}\right\}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left|F\left(s,Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v-\frac{\alpha}{t})+x-(t-s)v-\frac{s\alpha}{t}\right)\right|\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}\frac{dvds}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\frac{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}{\ t^{\sigma}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left|F\left(s,Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v-\frac{\alpha}{t})+x-(t-s)v-\frac{s\alpha}{t}\right)\right|\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}.

(6.12)

Then, applying Lemma
8.1
with
\mathcal{H}=F
and
\varphi(x,v)=Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v-\frac{\alpha}{t})-\frac{s\alpha}{t}
, one gets

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{4}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\underbrace{\frac{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}{\ t^{\sigma+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{t/2}\|F(s)\|_{L^{1}}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}}_{\text{Thanks to }\eqref{mathcal H 1}}+\underbrace{\frac{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}{\ t^{\sigma}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{t/2}^{t}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}}_{\text{Thanks to }\eqref{mathcal H p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\frac{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\sigma}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\|F(s)\|_{L^{1}}\right).

Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\sigma}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{4}[F,\eta](t)\|_{L^{p}}}{|\alpha|^{\sigma}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{1,\infty}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\right).

Summing up, we conclude that for
t\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1,\infty}t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+\sigma}\|\mathcal{T}[F,\eta](t)\|_{\dot{B}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\lesssim_{a,\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\sum_{p=1,\infty}\left(\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|F(s)\|_{L^{p}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a}\|F(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{p,\infty}}\right).

(6.13)

Hence, we proved the Lemma with (
6.8
) and (
6.13
).

Remark 6.2

For
a_{1}\in(0,1)
, if we also have
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a_{1}}}\lesssim 1
,

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{T}^{i}_{\alpha}[F,\eta](t,x)\right|\lesssim_{a_{1},\mathbf{c}_{1}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a_{1}}}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|F(s,x-(t-s)v)|\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a_{1}}}\frac{dvds}{\langle v\rangle^{N}},~~i=1,2.

(6.14)

Proposition 6.3

Let
a
,
\sigma
and
\rho
satisfy the same conditions in Lemma
6.1
. Then we have

\|\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)\|_{\sigma}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{1+\sigma}.

(6.15)

Proof.
Since
\langle v\rangle^{N}\sum_{j=0}^{2}\left|\nabla^{j}\left(\nabla\mu(v)\right)\right|\leq M^{*}
, thus, by Lemma
6.1
, we have

\|\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)\|_{\sigma}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{\sigma}\sum_{p=1,\infty}\left(\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|E\|_{L^{p}}+\sup_{s\in[0,t]}\langle s\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|E(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{\sigma}_{1,\infty}}\right)\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{1+\sigma}.

This implies the result.

Proposition 6.4

Let
a\in(\frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2},1)
and
\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{0}>0
be as in Proposition (
4.1
). Assume
\|(\rho_{1},U_{1})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}},\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\leq\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{0}
. There holds,

\|\mathcal{R}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{R}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{a}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\Big(\|(\rho_{1},U_{1})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{a}+\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\Big)\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

(6.16)

Proof.
In this proof, we recall the notations
X_{s,t}^{1}
,
X_{s,t}^{2}
,
Y_{s,t}^{1}
,
Y_{s,t}^{2}
,
V_{s,t}^{1}
,
V_{s,t}^{2}
,
\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}
,
\mathbf{W}_{s,t}
in Proposition
4.6
, and
X_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi),~Y_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi),~V_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi),W_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi)
in (
5.13
). Then we have

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})(t,x):=\mathcal{R}(\rho_{1},U_{1})(t,x)-\mathcal{R}(\rho_{2},U_{2})(t,x)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}(E^{1}-E^{2})(s,x-(t-s)v)\cdot\nabla_{v}\mu(v)dvds-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}(E^{1}-E^{2})(s,X_{s,t}^{1}(x,v))\cdot\nabla_{v}\mu(V_{s,t}^{1}(x,v))dvds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(E^{2}(s,X_{s,t}^{1}(x,v))-E^{2}(s,X_{s,t}^{2}(x,v))\right).\nabla\mu(V_{s,t}^{1}(x,v))

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}E^{2}(s,X_{s,t}^{2}(x,v)).\left(\nabla\mu(V_{s,t}^{1}(x,v))-\nabla\mu(V_{s,t}^{2}(x,v))\right)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle:\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathcal{T}[(E^{1}-E^{2})_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)+\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})(t,x)+\mathbf{R}^{2}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})(t,x).

Thanks to Lemma
6.1
with
F=(E^{1}-E^{2})_{i}
,
\eta=\partial_{i}\mu
and
\sigma=a
, we get

\sum_{i=1}^{d}\|\mathcal{T}[(E^{1}-E^{2})_{i},\partial_{i}\mu]\|_{a}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho_{1},U_{1})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{a}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

(6.17)

(1)
Estimate on
\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})(t,x)
, one has

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})(t,x)=-\sum_{i=1}^{d}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}(x-vt,v)\nabla E^{2}_{i}(s,X_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi))\partial_{i}\mu(V_{s,t}^{1}(x,v))d\varpi dsdv.

Hence,

\displaystyle|\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})(t,x)|\leq\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}\|\mathbf{Y}_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{|\nabla E^{2}(s,X_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi))|}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2+a}}|\nabla\mu(V_{s,t}^{1}(x,v))|dsdvd\varpi

\displaystyle\qquad\overset{\eqref{results of Y12 small}}{\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\langle s\rangle^{-d+2-a}|\nabla E^{2}(s,X_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi))|\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}dsdvd\varpi.

Note that
X_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi)=x-(t-s)v+Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v,\varpi)
, then we apply Lemma
8.2
with
\mathcal{H}=\nabla E^{2}
and
\varphi(x,v)=Y_{s,t}(x-tv,v,\varpi)
to obtain that for
t>1
,

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})(t,x)\|_{L^{p}}\\
&\quad\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{-d+2-a}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\nabla E^{2}(s,X_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi))\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}dv\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}dsd\varpi\\
&\quad\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\left(\int_{0}^{t/2}\langle s\rangle^{-d+2-a}t^{-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\left\|\nabla E^{2}(s)\right\|_{L^{1}_{x}}ds+\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{-d+2-a}\left\|\nabla E^{2}(s)\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}ds\right)\\
&\quad\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\frac{\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\left(\int_{0}^{t/2}+\int_{t/2}^{t}\right)\frac{\langle s\rangle^{2-a}}{\langle s\rangle^{d+a}}ds\lesssim\frac{\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}.\end{split}

(6.18)

Meanwhile, for
0\leq t\leq 1

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})(t,x)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{-d+2-a}\|\nabla E^{2}(s)\|_{L^{p}}ds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

(6.19)

Then, with (
6.19
) and (
6.18
), we can obtain

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1,\infty}\langle t\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\|\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2})\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

(6.20)

Now we deal with
\frac{|\delta_{\alpha}\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2})(t,x)|}{|\alpha|^{a}}
, for the case
t\leq 1
we have

\displaystyle\frac{|\delta_{\alpha}\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2})(t,x)|}{|\alpha|^{a}}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\langle s\rangle^{-d+2+a}|\nabla E^{2}(s,X_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi))|\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}dsd\varpi

\displaystyle\qquad+\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\langle s\rangle^{-d+2}\sup_{z}\frac{|\delta_{z}\nabla E^{2}(s,.)(X_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi))|}{|z|^{a}}\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}dsd\varpi

\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\langle s\rangle^{-d+2-a}|\nabla E^{2}(s,X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v,\varpi))|\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}dsd\varpi.

Then thanks to (
8.3
), we deduce that

\displaystyle\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2})\|_{L^{p}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}

\displaystyle\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{t}\left(\langle s\rangle^{-d+2-a}\|\nabla E^{2}(s)\|_{L^{p}}+\langle s\rangle^{-d+2}\|\nabla E^{2}(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{a}_{p,\infty}}\right)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}

\displaystyle\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{t}\left(\frac{\langle s\rangle^{-d+2-a}}{\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a}}+\frac{\langle s\rangle^{-d+2}}{\langle s\rangle^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+a}}\right)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}

\displaystyle\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

For the case
t>1
,
\frac{|\delta_{\alpha}\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})(t,x)|}{|\alpha|^{a}}
can be recast as

\displaystyle\frac{|\delta_{\alpha}\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})(t,x)|}{|\alpha|^{a}}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\frac{1}{t^{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\langle s\rangle^{-d+2}|\nabla E^{2}(s,X_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi))|\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}dsd\varpi

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{t^{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\langle s\rangle^{-d+2}\sup_{z}\frac{|\delta_{z}\nabla E^{2}(s,.)(X_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi))|}{|z|^{a}}\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}dsd\varpi

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{t^{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\langle s\rangle^{-d+2-a}|\nabla E^{2}_{i}(s,X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v,\varpi))|\left(\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}+\frac{1}{\langle v-\frac{\alpha}{t}\rangle^{N}}\right)dvdwd\varpi.

Thus, it holds that for
t>1
,

\displaystyle\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})\|_{L^{p}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\frac{1}{t^{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{0}^{t/2}\left(\langle s\rangle^{-d+2-a}\|\nabla E^{2}(s)\|_{L^{1}}+\langle s\rangle^{-d+2}\|\nabla E^{2}(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{a}_{1,\infty}}\right)ds

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{1}{t^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\int_{t/2}^{t}\left(\langle s\rangle^{-d+2-a}\|\nabla E^{2}(s)\|_{L^{p}}+\langle s\rangle^{-d+2}\|\nabla E^{2}(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{a}_{p,\infty}}\right)ds

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\frac{1}{t^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

Then,

\displaystyle\sum_{p=1,\infty}\langle t\rangle^{a+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\frac{\|\delta_{\alpha}\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})\|_{L^{p}}}{|\alpha|^{a}}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

(6.21)

Combining this with (
6.20
), one gets

\|\mathbf{R}^{1}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}.

(6.22)

(2)
Estimate on
\mathbf{R}^{2}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})(t,x)
.
We infer that

\displaystyle\mathbf{R}^{2}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})(t,x)

\displaystyle=-\sum_{i=1}^{d}\int_{0}^{1}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathbf{W}_{s,t}(x-vt,v)E^{2}_{i}(s,X_{s,t}^{2}(x,v))(\nabla\partial_{i}\mu)(V_{s,t}(x,v,\varpi))dvdsd\varpi.

Analogously, we obtain

\|\mathbf{R}^{2}(\rho_{1},\rho_{2},U_{1},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}},

(6.23)

which complete the proof.

Lemma 6.5

Let
H_{0}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}
and
A_{0}:\mathbb{R}^{d}\to\mathbb{R}^{d}
and
\lambda\in\mathbb{R}
. Then, for any muti-index
\alpha\not=0
,

\displaystyle|\partial^{\gamma}_{x}\left[H_{0}(\lambda x+A_{0}(x))\right]-(\partial_{x}^{\gamma}H_{0})(\lambda x+A_{0}(x))\lambda^{|\gamma|}|\lesssim|(\nabla^{|\gamma|}H_{0})(\lambda x+A_{0}(x))||\nabla A_{0}(x)|(|\lambda|+|\nabla A_{0}(x)|)^{|\gamma|-1}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad+\sum_{k=1}^{|\gamma|-1}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}\sum_{i=2}^{|\gamma|-j}|(\nabla^{k}H_{0})(\lambda x+A_{0}(x))|(|\lambda|+|\nabla A_{0}(x)|)^{j}|\nabla^{i}A_{0}(x)|^{\frac{|\gamma|-j}{i}}.

Proof.
Assume
\partial_{x}^{\gamma}=\partial_{x_{1}}^{\gamma_{1}}...\partial_{x_{d}}^{\gamma_{d}}
. We know that, for
\tilde{A}_{0}(x)=\lambda x+A_{0}(x)
, we have

\displaystyle\left|\partial^{\gamma}_{x}\left[H_{0}(\lambda x+A_{0}(x))\right]-\mathbf{Q}\right|\lesssim\sum_{k=1}^{|\gamma|-1}|(\nabla^{k}H_{0})(\tilde{A}_{0}(x))|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1},...,m_{k}\geq 1\\
m_{1}+..+m_{k}=|\gamma|\end{subarray}}\prod_{j=1}^{k}|\nabla^{m_{j}}\tilde{A}_{0}(x)|,

with

\mathbf{Q}=(\nabla^{|\gamma|}H_{0})(\lambda x+A_{0}(x)):\left[(\partial_{x_{1}}\tilde{A}_{0}(x))^{\otimes{\gamma_{1}}}\otimes(\partial_{x_{2}}\tilde{A}_{0}(x))^{\otimes{\gamma_{2}}}\otimes...\otimes(\partial_{x_{d}}\tilde{A}_{0}(x))^{\otimes{\gamma_{d}}}\right].

(6.24)

It is easy to check that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{|\gamma|-1}|(\nabla^{k}H_{0})(\tilde{A}_{0}(x))|\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}m_{1},...,m_{k}\geq 1\\
m_{1}+..+m_{k}=|\gamma|\end{subarray}}\prod_{j=1}^{k}|\nabla^{m_{j}}\tilde{A}_{0}(x)|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\sum_{k=1}^{|\gamma|-1}\sum_{j=0}^{k-1}\sum_{i=2}^{|\gamma|-j}|(\nabla^{k}H_{0})(\lambda x+A_{0}(x))|(|\lambda|+|\nabla A_{0}(x)|)^{j}|\nabla^{i}A_{0}(x)|^{\frac{|\gamma|-j}{i}},

and

\displaystyle\left|\mathbf{Q}-(\partial_{x}^{\gamma}H_{0})(\lambda x+A_{0}(x))\lambda^{|\gamma|}\right|\lesssim|(\nabla^{|\gamma|}H_{0})(\lambda x+A_{0}(x))||\nabla A_{0}(x)|(|\lambda|+|\nabla A_{0}(x)|)^{|\gamma|-1}.

(6.25)

Combining (
6.24
) with (
6.25
), we finish the proof.

Proposition 6.6

Let
a\in(\frac{\sqrt{5}-1}{2},1)
and assume
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon_{0}\leq\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{0}
with
\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{0}
mentioned in Proposition
4.1
. Then we have for
b\in(\frac{1+\delta_{0}}{2-\delta_{0}},1-2\delta_{0})
,

\|\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)\|_{l+1,b}\lesssim_{a,b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime},C_{A,l}}1,

(6.26)

provided
\|(\rho,U)\|_{l,1-\delta_{0}}\leq\mathbf{c}
,
\sum_{j=2}^{l+1}\sup_{|r|\leq 1}\left|{A^{(j)}(r)}\right|\leq C_{A,l}
and
\mu
satisfies

\sum_{i=1}^{l+3}\langle v\rangle^{N}\left|\nabla^{i}\mu(v)\right|\leq\mathbf{c}^{\prime},

(6.27)

where
l\geq 0
,
0<\delta_{0}<\frac{4-\sqrt{13}}{3}
and
N>d
.

Proof.
In this proof, we denote
C=C(a,b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime},C_{A,l})
.

(1)
We firstly consider
t\geq 1
.
Set

\displaystyle F_{1,1}(x)=\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)\left(Z_{1}(x)\right),~~\eta_{1,1}(x)=\partial_{i}\mu\left(Z_{2}\right),

\displaystyle F_{1,2}(x)=\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(w+\frac{s}{t}(x-w)),\eta_{1,2}(x)=\partial_{i}\mu(\frac{x-w}{t}),

\displaystyle F_{2,1}(x)=E_{i}\left(s,Z_{1}(x)\right),\eta_{2,1}(x)=\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\partial_{i}\mu\left(Z_{2}(x)\right),

\displaystyle F_{2,2}(x)=E_{i}(s,w+\frac{s}{t}(x-w)),~~\eta_{2,2}(x)=\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\partial_{i}\mu(\frac{x-w}{t}),

\displaystyle F_{3}(x)=E_{i}\left(s,Z_{1}(x-\alpha)\right)-E_{i}\left(s,Z_{4}(x)\right),~~\eta_{3}(x)=\partial_{i}\mu\left(Z_{3}(x)\right)-\partial_{i}\mu\left(Z_{2}(x)\right),

where
Z_{1},Z_{2},Z_{3},Z_{4}
are defined in (
6.9
) and
w=x-vt
, then we have,

\displaystyle\delta_{\alpha}\mathcal{T}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)=\sum_{j=1}^{4}\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{j}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x),

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{1}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(F_{1,1}(x)\eta_{1,1}(x)-F_{1,2}(x)\eta_{1,2}(x)\right)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{2}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(F_{2,1}(x)\eta_{2,1}(x)-F_{2,2}(x)\eta_{2,2}(x)\right)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{3}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F_{3}(x)\eta_{1,1}(x)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{4}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}F_{2,1}(x-\alpha)\eta_{3}(x)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}}.

Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle|\nabla^{l+1}_{x}\delta_{\alpha}\mathcal{T}^{j}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)|\lesssim\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}\left|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{j}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}](t,x)\right|,

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{1}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}](t,x)=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{1,1}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{1,1}(x)-\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{1,2}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{1,2}(x)\right)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{2}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{2,1}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{2,1}(x)-\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{2,2}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{2,2}(x)\right)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{3}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{3}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{1,1}(x)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}},

\displaystyle\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{4}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{2,1}(x-\alpha)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{3}(x)\frac{dwds}{t^{d}}.

1.1)
Estimate of terms in
\nabla_{x}^{l+1}\mathcal{T}^{1}_{\alpha}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu]
.

Note that

\displaystyle\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{1,1}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{1,1}(x)-\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{1,2}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{1,2}(x)

\displaystyle\quad=\left(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{1,1}(x)-\left(\frac{s}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{1}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})\right)\left(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{1,1}(x)-\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{2}|}(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{1,1}(x)-\left(\frac{s}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{1}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{2}|}(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{s}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{1}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})\left(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{1,1}(x)-\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{2}|}(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\left(\frac{s}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{1}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{2}|}(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})-\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{1,2}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{1,2}(x)\right)

\displaystyle\quad:=\sum_{k=1}^{4}\mathcal{T}^{1,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,k}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t}).

We first deal with the fourth term directly

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathcal{T}^{1,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,4}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t})\frac{dwds}{t^{d}}\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}=\frac{1}{t^{l+1}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}\left\|\mathcal{T}^{1}_{\alpha}\big[s^{|\beta_{1}|}\partial^{\beta_{1}}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i},\partial^{\beta_{2}}\partial_{i}\mu\big]\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\\
&\quad\overset{\eqref{T alpha lower 1}}{\lesssim}_{\delta_{0},\mathbf{c}}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{d+l+1+b}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=0}^{l+1}\left(\int_{0}^{t/2}|s|^{|\beta_{1}|+b}\|\nabla^{|\beta_{1}|}E_{i}(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{b}_{1,\infty}}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-1-\delta_{0}}}\right.\\
&\left.+\int_{t/2}^{t}\sup_{0\leq s\leq t}|s|^{|\beta_{1}|+b+d}\|\nabla^{|\beta_{1}|}E_{i}(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{b}_{\infty,\infty}}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-1-\delta_{0}}}\right)\\
&\quad\overset{\eqref{E estimate by rho'}}{\lesssim_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c},C_{A,l}}}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{d+l+1+b}}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{|s|^{l+1+b}}{\langle s\rangle^{(l+1-\delta_{0})+(d-1-\delta_{0})}}ds\lesssim_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c},C_{A,l}}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{d+l+1+b}},\end{split}

(6.28)

since
b<1-2\delta_{0}
.

Then we start to estimate
\mathcal{T}^{1,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,k}
, where
k=1,2,3.

Applying Lemma
6.5
and Proposition
4.4
,
4.4
, we get

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{1,1}(x)-\left(\frac{s}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{1}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})\right|\lesssim\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}|\nabla^{|\beta_{1}|}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})||\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}|\left(s+|\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}|\right)^{|\beta_{1}|-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|-1}\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}\sum_{i=2}^{|\beta_{1}|-j}|\nabla^{m}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|(|s|+|\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}|)^{j}|\nabla_{v}^{i}Y_{s,t}|^{\frac{|\beta_{1}|-j}{i}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}\left(\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}|\nabla^{|\beta_{1}|}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|\langle s\rangle^{|\beta_{1}|-d+\delta_{0}}+\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|-1}|\nabla^{m}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}|\nabla^{m}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1},

with
\nabla_{v}^{i}Y_{s,t}:=(\nabla_{v}^{i}Y_{s,t})(w,\frac{x-w}{t}).

Similarly, we also can estimate

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{1,1}(x)-\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{2}|}(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})\right|\lesssim\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{2}|\geq 1}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|}}|\nabla^{|\beta_{2}|}\partial_{i}\mu(Z_{2})||\nabla_{v}W_{s,t}|(1+|\nabla_{v}W_{s,t}|)^{|\beta_{2}|-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{2}|\geq 1}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{2}|-1}\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}\sum_{i=2}^{|\beta_{2}|-j}|\nabla^{m}\partial_{i}\mu(Z_{2})|(1+|\nabla_{v}W_{s,t}|)^{j}|\nabla_{v}^{i}W_{s,t}|^{\frac{|\beta_{2}|-j}{i}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{results of Y small},\eqref{proposition of higher derivative estimates Y W}}{\lesssim_{C}}\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{2}|\geq 1}\left(\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|}}|\nabla^{|\beta_{2}|}\partial_{i}\mu(Z_{2})|\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}}+\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|}}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{2}|}|\nabla^{m}\partial_{i}\mu(Z_{2})|\langle s\rangle^{-d+1}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\lesssim_{C}\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{2}|\geq 1}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|}}\frac{1}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}}\langle s\rangle^{-d+1}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}|\mathcal{T}^{1,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,1}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t})|\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{2}}{t^{l+1}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}|\nabla^{m}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|\langle s\rangle^{m-2d+2}\frac{1}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}},

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}|\mathcal{T}^{1,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,2}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t})|\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{2}}{t^{l+1}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}|\nabla^{m}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}\frac{1}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}},

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}|\mathcal{T}^{1,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,3}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t})|\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{2}}{t^{l+1}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=0}^{l}\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})\right|\langle s\rangle^{|\beta_{1}|-d+1}\frac{1}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}}.

Hence, we get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sum_{k=1}^{3}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}|\mathcal{T}^{1,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,k}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t})|\lesssim_{C}&\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{2}}{t^{l+1}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}|\nabla^{m}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}\frac{1}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}}.\end{split}

(6.29)

Note that we have (
8.1
) and (
8.2
) in the Appendix with
\mathcal{H}=\nabla^{m}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}
and
\varphi=Y_{s,t}(x,v)
, we make the change of variables
v=\frac{x-w}{t}
, then we apply (
4.15
) to obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sum_{k=1}^{3}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}|\mathcal{T}^{1,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,k}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t})|\frac{dwds}{t^{d}}\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\\
&\quad\lesssim_{C}\frac{1}{t^{l+1}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|\nabla^{m}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s,.)(X_{s,t}(x,v))|\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}dv\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}ds\\
&\quad\lesssim_{C}\frac{1}{t^{l+1+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}\int_{0}^{t/2}\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}\|\nabla^{m}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s)\|_{L^{1}}ds+\frac{1}{t^{l+1}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}\|\nabla^{m}\delta_{\frac{s\alpha}{t}}E_{i}(s)\|_{L^{p}}ds\\
&\quad\lesssim_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+l+1+b}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}\left(\int_{0}^{t/2}+\int_{t/2}^{t}\right)\frac{|s|^{b}\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}}{\langle s\rangle^{m+1-\delta_{0}}}ds\lesssim_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+l+1+b}}.\end{split}

(6.30)

Thanks to (

) and (

), we have

\displaystyle t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+l+1+b}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\left\|\nabla^{l+1}_{x}\mathcal{T}^{1}_{\alpha}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t)\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}\lesssim_{a,b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime},C_{A,l}}1.

(6.31)

1.2)
Estimate of terms in
\nabla_{x}^{l+1}\mathcal{T}^{2}_{\alpha}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu]
. It is clear that

\displaystyle\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{2,1}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{2,1}(x)-\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{2,2}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{2,2}(x)

\displaystyle\quad=\left(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{2,1}(x)-\left(\frac{s}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{1}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})\right)\left(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{2,1}(x)-\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{2}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{2,1}(x)-\left(\frac{s}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{1}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})\right)\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{2}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\frac{s}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{1}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})\left(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{2,1}(x)-\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{2}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\left(\left(\frac{s}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{1}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{2}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})-\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{2,2}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{2,2}(x)\right)

\displaystyle\quad:=\sum_{k=1}^{4}\mathcal{T}^{2,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,k}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t}).

Then, we also compute the last term first as follows

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathcal{T}^{2,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,4}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t})\frac{dwds}{t^{d}}\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}=\frac{1}{t^{l+1}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}\left\|\mathcal{T}^{2}_{\alpha}\big[s^{|\beta_{1}|}\partial^{\beta_{1}}E_{i},\partial^{\beta_{2}}\partial_{i}\mu\big]\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{T alpha lower 1}}{\lesssim_{\delta_{0},\mathbf{c}}}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{d+l+1+b}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=0}^{l+1}\left(\int_{0}^{t/2}|s|^{|\beta_{1}|}\|\nabla^{|\beta_{1}|}E_{i}(s)\|_{L^{1}}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-1-\delta_{0}}}+\int_{t/2}^{t}|s|^{|\beta_{1}|+d}\|\nabla^{|\beta_{1}|}E_{i}(s)\|_{L^{\infty}}\frac{ds}{\langle s\rangle^{d-1-\delta_{0}}}\right)

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{E estimate by rho'}}{\lesssim}_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c},C_{A,l}}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{d+l+1+b}}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{|s|^{l+1}}{\langle s\rangle^{(l+1-\delta_{0})+(d-1-\delta_{0})}}ds\lesssim_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c},C_{A,l}}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{d+l+1+b}}.

(6.32)

Now, we turn to
\mathcal{T}^{2,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,k}
, where
k=1,2,3.

Applying Proposition
4.4
, we can get

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{2,1}(x)-\left(\frac{s}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{1}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})\right|\lesssim\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}|\nabla^{|\beta_{1}|}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})||(\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t})|\left(s+|\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}|\right)^{|\beta_{1}|-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|-1}\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}\sum_{i=2}^{|\beta_{1}|-j}|\nabla^{m}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|(|s|+|\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}|)^{j}|\nabla_{v}^{i}Y_{s,t}|^{\frac{|\beta_{1}|-j}{i}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{results of Y small},\eqref{proposition of higher derivative estimates Y W}}{\lesssim_{C}}\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}\left(|\nabla^{|\beta_{1}|}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|\langle s\rangle^{|\beta_{1}|-d+\delta_{0}}+\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|-1}|\nabla^{m}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}|\nabla^{m}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1},

and

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{2,1}(x)-\left(\frac{1}{t}\right)^{|\beta_{2}|}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})\right|\lesssim\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{2}|\geq 1}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|}}|\nabla^{|\beta_{2}|}(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})||\nabla_{v}W_{s,t}|(1+|\nabla_{v}W_{s,t}|)^{|\beta_{2}|-1}

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{2}|\geq 1}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|}}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{2}|}\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}\sum_{i=2}^{|\beta_{2}|-j}|\nabla^{m}(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})|(1+|\nabla_{v}W_{s,t}|)^{j}|\nabla_{v}^{i}W_{s,t}|^{\frac{|\beta_{2}|-j}{i}}

\displaystyle\overset{\eqref{proposition of higher derivative estimates Y W}}{\lesssim_{C}}\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{2}|\geq 1}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|}}\left(|\nabla^{|\beta_{2}|}(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})|\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}}+\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{2}|-1}|\nabla^{m}(\delta_{\frac{\alpha}{t}}\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2})|\langle s\rangle^{-d+1}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{2}|\geq 1}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|+b}}\left(\frac{1}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}}+\frac{1}{\langle\frac{x-w-\alpha}{t}\rangle^{N}}\right)\langle s\rangle^{-d+1}.

Define

\Xi(x,w,\alpha,t)=\frac{1}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}}+\frac{1}{\langle\frac{x-w-\alpha}{t}\rangle^{N}}.

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}|\mathcal{T}^{2,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,1}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t})|\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{l+1+b}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}|\nabla^{m}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|\langle s\rangle^{m-2d+2}\Xi(x,w,\alpha,t),

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}|\mathcal{T}^{2,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,2}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t})|\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{l+1+b}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}|\nabla^{m}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}\Xi(x,w,\alpha,t),

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}|\mathcal{T}^{2,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,3}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t})|\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{l+1+b}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=0}^{l}\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})\right|\langle s\rangle^{|\beta_{1}|-d+1}\Xi(x,w,\alpha,t).

We observe to get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\sum_{k=1}^{3}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}|\mathcal{T}^{2,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,k}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t})|\lesssim_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{l+1+b}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}|\nabla^{m}E_{i}(s,.)(Z_{1})|\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}\Xi(x,w,\alpha,t).\end{split}

(6.33)

As the proof of (
6.30
), we have Remark
8.2
in the Appendix with

and

, we make the change of variable
