) to obtain

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\left\|\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sum_{k=1}^{3}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}|\mathcal{T}^{1,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}}_{\alpha,k}(t,x,\frac{x-w}{t})|\frac{dwds}{t^{d}}\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\\
&\quad\lesssim_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{l+1+b}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}|\nabla^{m}E_{i}(s,.)(X_{s,t}(x,v))|\left(\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}+\frac{1}{\langle v-\frac{\alpha}{t}\rangle^{N}}\right)dv\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}ds\\
&\quad\lesssim_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{l+1+b+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}\int_{0}^{t/2}\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}\|\nabla^{m}E_{i}(s)\|_{L^{1}}ds\\
&\qquad+\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{l+1+b}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}\int_{t/2}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}\|\nabla^{m}E_{i}(s)\|_{L^{p}}ds\\
&\quad\lesssim_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+l+1+b}}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}\left(\int_{0}^{t/2}+\int_{t/2}^{t}\right)\frac{|s|^{b}\langle s\rangle^{m-d+1}}{\langle s\rangle^{m+1-\delta_{0}}}ds\lesssim_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+l+1+b}}.\end{split}

(6.35)

1.3)
Estimate of terms in
\mathcal{T}^{3}_{\alpha}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu]
.

Firstly, we have

\displaystyle|\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}F_{3}(x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{1}|,|\vartheta_{i}|>0\\
|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{3}|}|=|\beta_{1}|\end{subarray}}\Bigg|\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{4}(x))(\partial^{\beta_{3}}E_{i})(s,Z_{4}(x))-\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{4}(x-\alpha))(\partial^{\beta_{3}}E_{i})(s,Z_{1}(x-\alpha))\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{1}|,|\vartheta_{i}|>0\\
|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{3}|}|=|\beta_{1}|\end{subarray}}\left|\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{4}(x))-\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{4}(x-\alpha))\right||(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i})(s,Z_{4}(x))|

\displaystyle\qquad+\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}|\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{4}(x-\alpha)|\left|(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i})(s,Z_{4}(x))-(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i})(s,Z_{1}(x-\alpha))\right|.

Then, by Proposition
4.1
and
4.4
,
for
|\beta_{1}|\geq 1
,

\displaystyle|\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}F_{3}(x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c}}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|+b}}\sum_{1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq\beta_{1}|}^{|\beta_{1}|}\left(\left[\langle s\rangle^{|\beta_{3}|-d+1}\mathbf{1}_{(|\beta_{3}|,|\beta_{1}|)\not=(1,l+1)}+\langle s\rangle^{-d+2+\delta_{0}+b}\mathbf{1}_{(|\beta_{3}|,|\beta_{1}|)=(1,l+1)}\right]|(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i})(s,Z_{4})\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+\langle s\rangle^{|\beta_{3}|-(d-1-\delta_{0})b-\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{3}|<|\beta_{1}|}}\sup_{z}\frac{\left|(\delta_{z}\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i})(s,Z_{4})\right|}{|z|^{b}}\right).

And if
|\beta_{1}|=0
, we can obtain

\displaystyle|F_{3}(x)|\leq\left|\frac{\alpha}{t}\right|^{b}\frac{|\delta_{z}E_{i}(s,\cdot)|}{|z|^{b}}\|\nabla_{v}Y_{s,t}\|_{L^{\infty}}^{b}\lesssim_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c}}\left|\frac{\alpha}{t}\right|^{b}\sup_{z}\frac{|\delta_{z}E_{i}(s,\cdot)|}{|z|^{b}}\langle s\rangle^{-b(d-1-\delta_{0})}.

Now we estimate the
L^{1}
and
L^{\infty}
norm of
\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}F_{3}(x)
as follows,

\displaystyle\|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{3}(x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim

{}_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c}}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|+b}}\sum_{1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq\beta_{1}|}\left[\langle s\rangle^{|\beta_{3}|-d+1}\mathbf{1}_{(|\beta_{3}|,|\beta_{1}|)\not=(1,l+1)}+\langle s\rangle^{-d+2+\delta_{0}+b}\mathbf{1}_{(|\beta_{3}|,|\beta_{1}|)=(1,l+1)}\right]\|(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i})(s)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle+\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|+b}}\left(\sum_{1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq\beta_{1}|}\langle s\rangle^{|\beta_{3}|-(d-1-\delta_{0})b-\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{3}|<|\beta_{1}|}}\|\nabla^{|\beta_{1}|}E_{i}(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{b}_{p,\infty}}+\|E_{i}(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{b}_{p,\infty}}\langle s\rangle^{-b(d-1-\delta_{0})}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

{}_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|+b}}\left(\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{d-\delta_{0}+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}+\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{d-b-2\delta_{0}+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}+\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{-\delta_{0}+(d-1-\delta_{0})b+\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim

{}_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|+b}}\langle s\rangle^{\delta_{0}-(d-1-\delta_{0})b-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}.

(6.36)

On the other hand, for
|\beta_{2}|\geq 0
, we have

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\eta_{1,1}(x)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t^{|\beta_{2}|}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{2}|,|\vartheta_{i}|>0\\
|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{3}|}|=|\beta_{2}|\end{subarray}}\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}|\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{2}(x)\|\partial^{\beta_{3}}\partial_{i}\mu|(Z_{2}(x))\lesssim_{C}\frac{1}{t^{|\beta_{2}|}}\frac{1}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}}.

Since
(d-1-\delta_{0})b-\delta_{0}>1
,
\delta_{0}\leq\frac{4-\sqrt{13}}{3}
, we apply (
6.36
) to get

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{3}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t)\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime}}&\int_{0}^{t/2}\frac{1}{t^{|\beta_{2}|}}\|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{3}(x)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}\frac{ds}{(t-s)^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}+\int_{t/2}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{1}{t^{|\beta_{2}|}}\|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{3}(x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}dvds\\
\lesssim_{C}&\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+l+1+b}}\left(\int_{0}^{t/2}+\int_{t/2}^{t}\right)\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{-\delta_{0}+(d-1-\delta_{0})b}}ds\lesssim_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+l+1+b}}.\end{split}

Thus, we have

\displaystyle t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+l+1+b}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\left\|\mathcal{T}^{3}_{\alpha}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t)\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}\lesssim_{C}1.

(6.37)

1.4)
Estimate of terms in
\mathcal{T}^{4}_{\alpha}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu]
.

Firstly, we have for
|\beta_{1}|\geq 1
,

\displaystyle|\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}F_{2,1}(x-\alpha)|\lesssim

\displaystyle\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{1}|-1,|\vartheta_{i}|>0\\
|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{3}|}|=|\beta_{1}|\end{subarray}}|\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{1}(x-\alpha))(\partial^{\beta_{3}}E_{i})(s,Z_{1}(x-\alpha))|

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{1}|}|=|\beta_{1}|,|\vartheta_{i}|>0}|\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{1}(x-\alpha))(\partial^{\beta_{1}}E_{i})(s,Z_{1}(x-\alpha))|

\displaystyle\lesssim

{}_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c}}\frac{\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}\sum_{1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{1}|}\langle s\rangle^{|\beta_{3}|-\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{3}|<|\beta_{1}|}}\left|(\partial^{\beta_{3}}E_{i})(s,Z_{1}(x-\alpha))\right|,

and for
|\beta_{1}|=0
, we obtain

\displaystyle|F_{2,1}(x-\alpha)|=\left|E_{i}(s,Z_{1}(x-\alpha))\right|.

Then, we estimate the
L^{1}
and
L^{\infty}
norm of
\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}F_{2,1}

\displaystyle\|\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}F_{2,1}\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c}}

\displaystyle\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}\sum_{1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{1}|}\langle s\rangle^{|\beta_{3}|-\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{3}|<|\beta_{1}|}}\left\|(\partial^{\beta_{3}}E_{i})(s,Z_{1}(x-\alpha))\right\|_{L^{p}}+\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|=0}\left\|E_{i}(s,Z_{1}(x-\alpha))\right\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle\frac{1}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}\langle s\rangle^{\delta_{0}-1-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}\lesssim_{C}\frac{1}{t^{|\beta_{1}|}}\langle s\rangle^{\delta_{0}-1-\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}.

(6.38)

Meanwhile, for
|\beta_{2}|\geq 1
, we have

\displaystyle|\partial^{\beta_{2}}_{x}\eta_{3}(x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{2}|,|\vartheta_{i}|>0\\
|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{3}|}|=|\beta_{2}|\end{subarray}}|\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{2}(x))(\partial^{\beta_{3}}\partial_{i}\mu)(s,Z_{2}(x))-\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{2}(x-\alpha))(\partial^{\beta_{3}}\partial_{i}\mu)(s,Z_{3}(x))|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{2}|,|\vartheta_{i}|>0\\
|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{3}|}|=|\beta_{2}|\end{subarray}}\left(\left|\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{2}(x))-\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{2}(x-\alpha))\right||(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}\partial_{i}\mu)(s,Z_{2}(x))|\right.

\displaystyle\quad\left.+\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}|\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}Z_{2}(x-\alpha)|\left|(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}\partial_{i}\mu)(s,Z_{2}(x))-(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}\partial_{i}\mu)(s,Z_{3}(x))\right|\right)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|+b}}\frac{\langle s\rangle^{-d+2}}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}}+\frac{\min\left\{\frac{|\alpha|}{t},1\right\}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|}}\frac{\langle s\rangle^{-d+2}}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}}\lesssim_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|+b}}\frac{\langle s\rangle^{-d+2}}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}}.

For
|\beta_{2}|=0
, we have

\displaystyle|\eta_{3}(x)|=

\displaystyle\left|(\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{2}(x))-(\partial_{i}\mu)(Z_{3}(x))\right|=\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{b}}\frac{|\partial_{i}\mu(Z_{2}(x))-\partial_{i}\mu(Z_{3}(x))|}{|Z_{2}(x)-Z_{3}(x)|^{b}}\left|\frac{Z_{2}(x)-Z_{3}(x)}{|\alpha|/t}\right|^{b}

\displaystyle\lesssim

{}_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}^{b}}{t^{b}}\langle s\rangle^{(\delta_{0}-d)b}\frac{1}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}}\lesssim_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{b}}\frac{\langle s\rangle^{(\delta_{0}-d)b}}{\langle\frac{x-w}{t}\rangle^{N}}.

Thus, with (
6.38
), we have

\displaystyle\left\|\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}^{4}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)\right\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=0}^{l}\int_{0}^{t/2}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|+b}}\langle s\rangle^{-d+2}\|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{2,1}(x)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}\frac{ds}{(t-s)^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t/2}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{b}}\langle s\rangle^{(\delta_{0}-d)b}\|\partial_{x}^{l+1}F_{2,1}(x)\|_{L^{1}_{x}}\frac{ds}{(t-s)^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}

\displaystyle+\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=0}^{l}\int_{0}^{t/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{|\beta_{2}|+b}}\frac{\langle s\rangle^{-d+2}}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}\|\nabla_{x}^{\beta_{1}}F_{2,1}(x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}dvds

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t/2}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{b}}\frac{\langle s\rangle^{(\delta_{0}-d)b}}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}\|\partial_{x}^{l+1}F_{2,1}(x)\|_{L^{p}_{x}}dvds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+l+1+b}}\left(\int_{0}^{t/2}+\int_{t/2}^{t}\right)\left(\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{d-1-\delta_{0}}}+\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{(d-\delta_{0})b+1-\delta_{0}}}\right)ds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+l+1+b}}.

This implies

\displaystyle t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}+l+1+b}\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\left\|\mathcal{T}^{4}_{\alpha}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t)\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}\lesssim_{C}1.

(6.39)

Hence, combining (
6.31
), (
6.35
), (
6.37
) with (
6.39
) we obtain

\displaystyle t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}l+1+b}\|\nabla^{l+1}_{x}\mathcal{T}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t)\|_{\dot{B}^{b}_{p,\infty}}\lesssim_{C}1.

(6.40)

(2)
We now consider
t\leq 1
.
Denote

\displaystyle\overline{F}_{1,1}(x)=\delta_{\alpha}E_{i}(s,.)\left(X_{s,t}(x,v)\right),~~\overline{\eta}_{1,1}(x)=\partial_{i}\mu\left(V_{s,t}(x,v)\right),

\displaystyle\overline{F}_{1,2}(x)=\delta_{\alpha}E_{i}(s,.)(x-(t-s)v),~~\overline{\eta}_{1,2}(x)=\partial_{i}\mu(v),

\displaystyle\overline{F}_{2,1}(x)=E_{i}\left(s,X_{s,t}(x,v)\right),~~\overline{F}_{3}(x)=E_{i}\left(s,X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v)\right)-E_{i}\left(s,X_{s,t}(x,v)-\alpha\right),

\displaystyle\overline{\eta}_{3}(x)=\partial_{i}\mu\left(V_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v)\right)-\partial_{i}\mu\left(V_{s,t}(x,v)\right).

Thus, we have

\displaystyle\delta_{\alpha}\mathcal{T}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)=\sum_{j=1}^{3}\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{j}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x),

with

\displaystyle\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{1}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(\overline{F}_{1,1}(x)\overline{\eta}_{1,1}(x)-\overline{F}_{1,2}(x)\overline{\eta}_{1,2}(x)\right)dvds,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{2}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\overline{F}_{3}(x)\overline{\eta}_{1,1}(x)dvds,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{3}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\overline{F}_{2,1}(x-\alpha)\overline{\eta}_{3}(x)dvds.

Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle|\nabla^{l+1}_{x}\delta_{\alpha}\overline{\mathcal{T}}^{j}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)|\lesssim\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}\left|\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{j}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}](t,x)\right|,

where

\displaystyle\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{1}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}](t,x)=-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\overline{F}_{1,1}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\overline{\eta}_{1,1}(x)-\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\overline{F}_{1,2}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\overline{\eta}_{1,2}(x)\right)dvds,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathcal{T}_{\alpha}}^{2}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\overline{F}_{3}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\overline{\eta}_{1,1}(x)dvds,

\displaystyle\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{3}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu,\beta_{1},\beta_{2}](t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\overline{F}_{2,1}(x-\alpha)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\overline{\eta}_{3}(x)dvds.

2.1)
Estimate of
\overline{\mathcal{T}}^{1}_{\alpha}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu]
.

We can write

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\overline{F}_{1,1}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\overline{\eta}_{1,1}(x)-\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\overline{F}_{1,2}(x)\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\overline{\eta}_{1,2}(x)\right|

\displaystyle\quad\leq\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\overline{F}_{1,1}(x)-\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\delta_{\alpha}E_{i}(s,.)(X_{s,t}(x,v))\right|\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\overline{\eta}_{1,1}(x)-(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\partial_{i}\mu)(V_{s,t}(x,v))\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\overline{F}_{1,1}(x)-\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\delta_{\alpha}E_{i}(s,.)(X_{s,t}(x,v))\right|\left|(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\partial_{i}\mu)(V_{s,t}(x,v))\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\delta_{\alpha}E_{i}(s,.)(X_{s,t}(x,v))\right|\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\overline{\eta}_{1,1}(x)-(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\partial_{i}\mu)(V_{s,t}(x,v))\right|

\displaystyle\qquad+\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\delta_{\alpha}E_{i}(s,.)(X_{s,t}(x,v))(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\partial_{i}\mu)(V_{s,t}(x,v))-\overline{F}_{1,2}(x)\overline{\eta}_{1,2}(x)\right|.

Thanks to Lemma
6.5
we have

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\overline{F}_{1,1}(x)-\partial_{x}^{\beta_{1}}\delta_{\alpha}F(s,.)(X_{s,t}(x,v))\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}|\nabla^{|\beta_{1}|}\delta_{\alpha}F(s,.)(X_{s,t}(x,v))||(\nabla_{x}Y_{s,t})|\left(1+|\nabla_{x}Y_{s,t}|\right)^{|\beta_{1}|-1}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|-1}\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}\sum_{i=2}^{|\beta_{1}|-j}|\nabla^{m}\delta_{\alpha}F(s,.)(X_{s,t}(x,v))|(1+|\nabla_{x}Y_{s,t}|)^{j}|\nabla_{x}^{i}Y_{s,t}|^{\frac{|\beta_{1}|-j}{i}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{1}|\geq 1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{1}|}|\nabla^{m}\delta_{\alpha}F(s,.)(X_{s,t}(x,v))|\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}}.

Then, for
|\beta_{2}|\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\left|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\overline{\eta}_{1,1}(x)-(\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\partial_{i}\mu)(V_{s,t}(x,v))\right|

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{2}|\geq 1}\Bigg(|\nabla^{|\beta_{2}|}\partial_{i}\mu(V_{s,t}(x,v))||\nabla_{x}W_{s,t}|^{|\beta_{2}|}+\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{2}|}\sum_{j=0}^{m-1}\sum_{i=2}^{|\beta_{2}|-j}|\nabla^{m}\partial_{i}\mu(V_{s,t}(x,v))\|\nabla_{x}W_{s,t}|^{j}|\nabla_{x}^{i}W_{s,t}|^{\frac{|\beta_{2}|-j}{i}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\overset{\eqref{results of Y small},\eqref{proposition of higher derivative estimates Y W}}{\lesssim_{C}}\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{2}|\geq 1}\sum_{m=1}^{|\beta_{2}|}|\nabla^{m}\partial_{i}\mu(V_{s,t}(x,v))|\langle s\rangle^{-d-1+\delta_{0}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon_{0}}}_{a}}\lesssim_{C}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{|\beta_{2}|\geq 1}}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}\langle s\rangle^{-1-d+\delta_{0}}.

Then, we have for
p=1,\infty
,

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{\mathcal{T}}^{1}_{\alpha}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu]\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle\sum_{|\beta_{1}|=0}^{l+1}\sum_{m=0}^{|\beta_{1}|}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\|\nabla^{m}\delta_{\alpha}E_{i}(s)\|_{L^{p}}\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}}\frac{dvds}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}+\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+|\beta_{2}|=l+1}\|\mathcal{T}[\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}\delta_{\alpha}E_{i},\partial^{\beta_{2}}_{x}\partial_{i}\mu]\|_{L^{p}_{x}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{b}\sum_{|\beta_{1}|+1}^{l+1}\sum_{m=0}^{|\beta_{1}|}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{m+1-\delta_{0}}}\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}}ds+\|\nabla^{|\beta_{1}|}E_{i}(s)\|_{\dot{B}^{b}_{p,\infty}}\right)\lesssim_{C}|\alpha|^{b}.

(6.41)

2.2)
Estimate of
\overline{\mathcal{T}}^{2}_{\alpha}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu]
.

Firstly, we consider
\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}\overline{F}_{3}(x)
as follows.
If
|\beta_{1}|\geq 1
, we get

\displaystyle|\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}\overline{F}_{3}(x)|\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{1}|,|\vartheta_{i}|>0\\
|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{3}|}|=|\beta_{1}|\end{subarray}}\Bigg|\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}X_{s,t}(x,v))(\partial^{\beta_{3}}E_{i})(s,X_{s,t}(x,v)-\alpha)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad-\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v))(\partial^{\beta_{3}}E_{i})(s,X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v))\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{1}|,|\vartheta_{i}|>0\\
|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{3}|}|=|\beta_{1}|\end{subarray}}\Bigg(\left|\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}X_{s,t}(x,v))-\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v))\right||(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i})(s,X_{s,t}(x,v)-\alpha)|

\displaystyle\quad+\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}|\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v)|\left|(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i})(s,X_{s,t}(x,v)-\alpha)-(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i})(s,X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v))\right|\Bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}|\alpha|^{b}\sum_{1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq\beta_{1}|}\left(\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}}|\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i}|(s,X_{s,t}(x,v)-\alpha)+\langle s\rangle^{-(d-\delta_{0})b}\sup_{z}\frac{\left|\delta_{z}\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i}(s,\cdot)(X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v))\right|}{|z|^{b}}\right).

If
|\beta_{1}|=0
, we can yield

\displaystyle|\overline{F}_{3}(x)|\leq\frac{|\overline{F}_{3}(x)|}{|\delta_{\alpha}Y_{s,t}(x,v)|^{b}}\left\|\nabla_{x}Y_{s,t}\right\|_{L^{\infty}}^{b}\lesssim_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c}}|\alpha|^{b}\sup_{z}\frac{|\delta_{z}E_{i}(s,\cdot)(X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v)|}{|z|^{b}}\langle s\rangle^{-(d-\delta_{0})b}.

Then, we consider another term. For
|\beta_{2}|\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|\partial_{x}^{\beta_{2}}\overline{\eta}_{1,1}(x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{2}|,|\vartheta_{i}|>0\\
|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{3}|}|=|\beta_{2}|,\end{subarray}}\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}|\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}W_{s,t}(x-vt,v)\|\partial^{\beta_{3}}\partial_{i}\mu|(V_{s,t}(x,v))|\lesssim_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime}}\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}.

Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle\|\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{2}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}|\alpha|^{b}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{1}|\leq l+1\\
1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{1}|\end{subarray}}\left(\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}}\|(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i})(s)\|_{L^{p}}+\langle s\rangle^{-(d-\delta_{0})b}\|\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}E_{i}\|_{\dot{F}^{b}_{p,\infty}}\right)\frac{dvds}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}

\displaystyle\quad+|\alpha|^{b}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\|E_{i}(s)\|_{\dot{F}^{b}_{p,\infty}}\langle s\rangle^{-(d-\delta_{0})b}\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}dvds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}|\alpha|^{b}\int_{0}^{t}\left(\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{d-2\delta_{0}+2}}+\frac{1}{\langle s\rangle^{(d-\delta_{0})b+2-\delta_{0}}}\right)ds\lesssim_{C}|\alpha|^{b}.

(6.42)

2.3)
Estimate of
\overline{\mathcal{T}}^{3}_{\alpha}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu]
.

Firstly, we estimate
\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}\overline{F}_{2,1}(x-\alpha)
. If
|\beta_{1}|\geq 1
,

\displaystyle|\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}\overline{F}_{2,1}(x-\alpha)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{1}|,|\vartheta_{i}|>0\\
|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{3}|}|=|\beta_{1}|\end{subarray}}|\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v))(\partial^{\beta_{3}}E_{i})(s,X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v))|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}\sum_{1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{1}|}\left|(\partial^{\beta_{3}}E_{i})(s,X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v))\right|.

Combining above with the definition of
\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}\overline{F}_{2,1}(x-\alpha)
, we get for any
|\beta_{1}|\geq 0
,

\displaystyle|\partial^{\beta_{1}}_{x}\overline{F}_{2,1}(x-\alpha)|\lesssim_{C}\sum_{0\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{1}|}\left|(\partial^{\beta_{3}}E_{i})(s,X_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v))\right|.

Then, we compute the other term.
For
|\beta_{2}|\geq 1
,

\displaystyle|\partial^{\beta_{2}}_{x}\overline{\eta}_{3}(x)|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq\beta_{2}|,|\vartheta_{i}|>0\\
|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{3}|}|=|\beta_{2}|\end{subarray}}\left|\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}W_{s,t}(x-vt,v)(\partial^{\beta_{3}}\partial_{i}\mu)(V_{s,t}(x,v))\right.

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left.-\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}W_{s,t}(x-\alpha-vt,v)(\partial^{\beta_{3}}\partial_{i}\mu)(V_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v))\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq\beta_{2}|,|\vartheta_{i}|>0\\
|\vartheta_{1}|+...+|\vartheta_{|\beta_{3}|}|=|\beta_{2}|\end{subarray}}\left|\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}W_{s,t}(x-vt,v))-\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}(\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}W_{s,t}(x-\alpha-vt,v))\right||(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}\partial_{i}\mu)(V_{s,t}(x,v))|

\displaystyle\quad+\prod_{j=1}^{|\beta_{3}|}|\partial_{x}^{\vartheta_{j}}W_{s,t}(x-\alpha-vt,v)|\left|(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}\partial_{i}\mu)(V_{s,t}(x,v))-(\nabla^{|\beta_{3}|}\partial_{i}\mu)(V_{s,t}(x-\alpha,v))\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}|\alpha|^{b}\frac{\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}-1}}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}+\min\{|\alpha|,1\}\left(\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}+\frac{1}{\langle v-\alpha\rangle^{N}}\right)\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}-1}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}|\alpha|^{b}\left(\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}+\frac{1}{\langle v-\alpha\rangle^{N}}\right)\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}-1}.

If
|\beta|_{2}=0
, we have

\displaystyle|\overline{\eta}_{3}(x)|\lesssim_{C}\frac{|\alpha|^{b}}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}-1}.

Hence, we deduce that

\displaystyle\left\|\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}^{3}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t,x)\right\|_{L^{p}}\lesssim_{b,\delta_{0},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime}}

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{b}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}0\leq|\beta_{1}|\leq l\\
0\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq|\beta_{1}|\end{subarray}}\left\|(\partial^{\beta_{3}}E_{i})(s)\right\|_{L^{p}}\left(\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}+\frac{1}{\langle v-\alpha\rangle^{N}}\right)\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}-1}dvds

\displaystyle+|\alpha|^{b}\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\sum_{1\leq|\beta_{3}|\leq l+1}\left\|(\partial^{\beta_{3}}E_{i})(s)\right\|_{L^{p}}\langle s\rangle^{\delta_{0}-d-1}\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}dvds

\displaystyle\lesssim_{C}

\displaystyle|\alpha|^{b}\int_{0}^{t}\langle s\rangle^{-d+\delta_{0}-1}ds\lesssim_{C}|\alpha|^{b}.

(6.43)

Thus, combining (
6.41
), (
6.42
) with (
6.43
), we have for
0\leq t\leq 1
,

\displaystyle\sup_{\alpha}\frac{\|\overline{\mathcal{T}}_{\alpha}[E_{i},\partial_{i}\mu](t)\|_{L^{\infty}}}{|\alpha|^{b}}\lesssim_{C}1.

(6.44)

In conclusion, thanks to (
6.40
) and (
6.44
), we finish the proof.

7
Proof of the Theorem
2.2
and Theorem
2.3

Recall the operator
\mathcal{F}
as:

\mathcal{F}(\rho,U)=\Big(\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U),\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho,U)\Big),

where

\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U)=G*_{(t,x)}(\mathcal{I}(\rho,U)+\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)+A(U))+\mathcal{I}(\rho,U)+\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)
and
\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho,U)=(1-\Delta)^{-1}(\rho+A(U)).

Now we start to prove Theorem
2.2
and
2.3
.

Proof of Theorem
2.2
.
Let
\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{0}
be in Proposition
4.1
and take
\varepsilon\leq\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{0}
. Assume

\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}},\|(\rho_{1},U_{1})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}},\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}\leq\varepsilon.

Applying Theorem
3.8
with
\mathbf{f}=\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho,U)+\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)
and
\mathbf{f}=\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho_{2},U_{2})+\mathcal{R}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{R}(\rho_{2},U_{2})
, there exists a constant
M=M(\text{\r{c}},d,M^{*})
such that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U)\|_{a}\leq M\Big(\|(\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}+\mathcal{R})(\rho,U)\|_{a}+\|A(U)\|_{a}\Big),

(7.1)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{a}\leq M\bigg(\|(\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}+\mathcal{R})(\rho_{1},U_{1})-(\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}+\mathcal{R})(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{a}+\|A(U_{1})-A(U_{2})\|_{a}\bigg).

(7.2)

Thanks to Lemma
4.2
, Proposition
5.1
, Proposition
6.3
and (
4.6
), we get that there exsists a constant
C_{3,1}=C_{3,1}(a,\text{\r{c}},d,M^{*})>0
and
C_{3,2}=C_{3,2}(a,d)>0
such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}\|\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U)\|_{a}\leq&C_{3,1}\left(\sum_{p=1,\infty}\left\|\mathcal{D}^{a}(f_{0})\right\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}}+\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}^{1+a}+C_{A}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}\right)\\
\leq&C_{3,1}\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{C_{2}}+\varepsilon^{1+a}+{\varepsilon}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\right)\leq\left(\frac{C_{3,1}}{C_{2}}+C_{3,1}(1+C_{A})\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\right)\varepsilon,\end{split}

(7.3)

and

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho,U)\|_{a}\leq

\displaystyle C_{3,2}\|\rho+A(U)\|_{a}\leq C_{3,2}\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}\leq C_{3,2}\varepsilon,

(7.4)

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{a}\leq

\displaystyle C_{3,2}\|\rho_{1}-\rho_{2}+A(U_{1})-A(U_{2})\|_{a}\leq C_{3,2}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}.

(7.5)

Moreover, with Proposition
5.4
and Proposition
6.4
, there exists a constant
C_{4}=C_{4}(a,\text{\r{c}},d,M^{*})>0
such that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\|\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{a}\leq C_{4}\|A(U_{1})-A(U_{2})\|_{a}\\
&+C_{4}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}\left(\sum_{p=1,\infty}\|\mathcal{D}^{a}(\nabla_{x,v}f_{0})\|_{L^{1}_{x}L^{p}_{v}\cap L^{1}_{v}L^{p}_{x}}+\|(\rho_{1},U_{1})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}^{a}+\|(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}\right)\\
&\leq\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}\left(\frac{C_{4}}{C_{2}}+2C_{4}\right){\varepsilon}^{a}+{C_{4}}C_{A}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}\\
&\leq\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}\left(\frac{C_{4}}{C_{2}}+(2+C_{A})C_{4}\right)\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}.\end{split}

(7.6)

Combining (
7.3
), (
7.6
), (
7.5
) with (
7.4
), we obtain that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}=

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U)\|_{a}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho,U)\|_{a}\leq\left(\frac{C_{3,1}}{C_{2}}+(C_{3,1}(1+C_{A})+C_{3,2})\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\right)\varepsilon,

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{F}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}=

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{a}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{a}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{a}\left(\frac{C_{4}}{C_{2}}+(2+C_{A})C_{4}+C_{3,2}\right)\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}

\displaystyle:=

\displaystyle C_{5}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{a}.

(7.7)

Hence, we choose
C_{2}=\max\{1,8C_{3,1},8C_{4}\}
, and
\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1}=\min\left\{\left(\frac{1}{4(C_{3,1}(1+C_{A})+C_{3,2})}\right)^{3},\left(\frac{1}{100+4((2+C_{A})C_{4}+C_{3,2})}\right)^{3},\tilde{\varepsilon}_{0}\right\},
we can yield that

\|\mathcal{F}(\rho,U)\|_{a}\leq\varepsilon,~~~~~~\|\mathcal{F}(\rho_{1},U_{1})-\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho_{2},U_{2})\|_{a}\leq\frac{1}{2}\|(\rho_{1}-\rho_{2},U_{1}-U_{2})\|_{a},~~\forall~\varepsilon\leq\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1}.

(7.8)

Therefore, we conclude that
\mathcal{F}
is a compressed mapping in
\mathfrak{B}(\varepsilon)
for
\varepsilon\leq\tilde{\varepsilon}_{1}

Now we consider the iterative sequence
(\varrho_{n},\mathcal{U}_{n})=\mathcal{F}(\varrho_{n-1},\mathcal{U}_{n-1})
with
\varrho_{0}(t,x)=U_{0}(t,x)=0
. So,
\varrho_{1}(t,x)=\mathcal{F}_{1}(0,0)(t,x)=G*_{t,x}\tilde{\varrho}_{0}(t,x)+\tilde{\varrho}_{0}(t,x)
and
\mathcal{U}_{1}=0
with
\tilde{\varrho}_{0}(t,x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}f_{0}(x-tv,v)dv
. From (
7.8
) we obtain that for
n\in\mathbb{N}
,

\|\mathcal{F}(\varrho_{n+1},\mathcal{U}_{n+1})-\mathcal{F}(\varrho_{n},\mathcal{U}_{n})\|_{a}\leq C_{5}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|(\varrho_{n}-\varrho_{n-1},\mathcal{U}_{n}-\mathcal{U}_{n-1})\|_{a}=C_{5}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|\mathcal{F}(\varrho_{n},\mathcal{U}_{n})-\mathcal{F}(\varrho_{n-1},\mathcal{U}_{n-1})\|_{a}.

(7.9)

Thus
\{(\varrho_{n},\mathcal{U}_{n})\}_{n=1}^{\infty}
is a Cauchy sequence. We denote

(\rho,U)=\Big(\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}(\varrho_{n}-\varrho_{n-1})+\varrho_{1},\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}(\mathcal{U}_{n}-\mathcal{U}_{n-1})+\mathcal{U}_{1}\Big),

it is clear that
\Big(\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U),\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho,U)\Big)=(\rho,U)\in\mathfrak{B}(\varepsilon).
Moreover by (
7.9
), we have

\displaystyle\|(\rho-\varrho_{1},U-\mathcal{U}_{1})\|_{a}\leq\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\|(\varrho_{n}-\varrho_{n-1},\mathcal{U}_{n}-\mathcal{U}_{n-1})\|_{a}

\displaystyle\qquad=\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}\|\mathcal{F}(\varrho_{n-1},\mathcal{U}_{n-1})-\mathcal{F}(\varrho_{n-2},\mathcal{U}_{n-2})\|_{a}\leq\varepsilon\sum_{n=2}^{\infty}(C_{5}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}})^{n-1}\leq 2C_{5}\varepsilon^{\frac{4}{3}}.

Recall the definition of
\mathcal{F}(\rho)
in (
2.7
), we have that the fixed point is exactly the solution of (
2.5
).
Now we consider
\|(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}
, note that

\displaystyle\|U\|_{1,a}

\displaystyle=\|\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho)\|_{1,a}\leq\|\rho\|_{1,a}+C_{A}\|U\|_{a}\|U\|_{1,a}\leq\|\rho\|_{1,a}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|U\|_{1,a},

\displaystyle\|\rho\|_{1,a}

\displaystyle=\|\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}=\Big\|G*_{(t,x)}\Big(\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho,U)+\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)+A(U)\Big)+\Big(\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho,U)+\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)\Big)\Big\|_{1,a}

\displaystyle\leq M\left(\|\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}+\|\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}+\|A(U)\|_{1,a}\right)

\displaystyle\leq M\left(\|\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}+\|\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|U\|_{1,a}\right).

Therefore we have

\displaystyle\|\rho\|_{1,a}+\frac{1}{4}\|U\|_{1,a}\leq M\left(\|\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}+\|\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}\right)+M\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|U\|_{1,a}+\frac{1}{4}\|\rho\|_{1,a}+\frac{1}{4}\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|U\|_{1,a},

(7.10)

which implies that

\displaystyle\|\rho\|_{1,a}+\|U\|_{1,a}\leq 8M\left(\|\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}+\|\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}\right).

Thanks to (
5.2
) and (
6.15
), we get

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho,U)\|_{1-\delta_{1}}+\|\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)\|_{1-\delta_{1}}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},a,M^{*}}1,

where
\delta_{1}=\min\{\frac{4-\sqrt{13}}{9},\frac{1-a}{3}\}
.
Then we use Proposition
6.26
and (
5.3
) to obtain

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}+\|\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},a,M^{*}}1.

Consequently, it gives that

\|(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},a,M^{*}}1.

The proof is complete.

Proof of Theorem
2.3
.
From Theorem
2.2
, we know that
\|(\rho,U)\|_{S^{{\varepsilon}}_{a}}\leq\widetilde{\varepsilon}_{0}
and
\|(\rho,U)\|_{1,a}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},a,M^{*}}1
. Applying the Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequality, we get
\|(\rho,U)\|_{1-\delta_{0}}\lesssim_{a,M^{*}}1
, where
\delta_{0}\in(0,\min\{\frac{4-\sqrt{13}}{3^{m+1}},\frac{1-b}{3^{m}}\})
.
Combining Theorem
3.8
and Proposition
5.12
with Proposition
6.6
, one has

\displaystyle\|\rho\|_{1,1-3\delta_{0}}=\|\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U)\|_{1,1-3\delta_{0}}\leq M\left(\|\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho,U)\|_{1,1-3\delta_{0}}+\|\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)\|_{1,1-3\delta_{0}}+\|A(U)\|_{1,1-3\delta_{0}}\right),

\displaystyle\|U\|_{1,1-3\delta_{0}}=\|\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho,U)\|_{1,1-3\delta_{0}}\leq\|\rho\|_{1,a}+\|A(U)\|_{1,1-3\delta_{0}}\leq\|\rho\|_{1,a}+\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{3}}\|U\|_{1,a},

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho)\|_{1,1-3\delta_{0}}+\|\mathcal{R}(\rho)\|_{1,1-3\delta_{0}}\lesssim_{a,M^{*},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime}}1.

Using the same method in (
7.10
) we have

\|(\rho,U)\|_{1,1-3\delta_{0}}=\|(\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U),\mathcal{F}_{2}(\rho,U))\|_{1,1-3\delta_{0}}\lesssim_{a,M^{*},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime},C_{A,m}}1.

Note that

\displaystyle\|\langle s\rangle^{2}\nabla^{2}(A(U))\|_{\dot{B}^{1-9\delta_{0}}_{p,\infty}}\leq C_{A,m}\|U\|_{1,1-9\delta_{0}}+C_{A}\varepsilon^{\frac{2}{3}}\|\langle s\rangle^{2}\nabla^{2}U\|_{\dot{B}^{1-9\delta_{0}}_{p,\infty}}

We do one more step to obtain

\displaystyle\|(\rho,U)\|_{2,1-9\delta_{0}}

\displaystyle=\|(\mathcal{F}_{1}(\rho,U),\mathcal{F}(\rho,U))\|_{2,1-9\delta_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},a,M^{*},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime},C_{A,m}}\|\mathcal{I}_{f_{0}}(\rho,U)\|_{2,1-9\delta_{0}}+\|\mathcal{R}(\rho,U)\|_{2,1-9\delta_{0}}+\|(\rho,U)\|_{2,1-9\delta_{0}}

\displaystyle\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},a,M^{*},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime},C_{A,m}}1.

Then, with
m
times iteration, we have

\|(\rho,U)\|_{m,1-3^{m}\delta_{0}}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},a,M^{*},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime},C_{A,m}}1.

Note that
1-3^{m}\delta_{0}>b
, thus by the Gagliardo-Nirenberg interpolation inequality again to get

\|(\rho,U)\|_{m,b}\lesssim_{\text{\r{c}},a,M^{*},\mathbf{c},\mathbf{c}^{\prime},C_{A,m}}1,

which completes the proof.

8
Appendix

Lemma 8.1

Assume
\mathcal{H}\in L^{1}(\mathbb{R}^{d})\cap L^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{d})
and
\varphi(x,v)
satisfy
\|\nabla_{x,v}\varphi(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\leq\frac{1}{2}
, then for
p=1,\infty
,
0\leq s\leq t
and
t\geq 0
we have

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathcal{H}\left(\varphi(x,v)+x-(t-s)v\right)\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim\|\mathcal{H}\|_{L^{p}}.

(8.1)

Moreover, for
0\leq s\leq\frac{t}{2}
and
t\geq 1
, we also get

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathcal{H}\left(\varphi(x,v)+x-(t-s)v\right)\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\|\mathcal{H}\|_{L^{1}}.

(8.2)

Proof.
We can directly prove the first inequality as follows

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathcal{H}\left(\varphi(x,v)+x-(t-s)v\right)\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\leq\|\mathcal{H}\|_{L^{p}}\left(1+\|\nabla_{x,v}\varphi(x,v)\|_{L^{\infty}_{x,v}}\right)\lesssim\|\mathcal{H}\|_{L^{p}}.

Furthermore, we change variable
\zeta=x-(t-s)v
and obtain for
0\leq s\leq\frac{t}{2}
,

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathcal{H}\left(\varphi(x,v)+x-(t-s)v\right)\frac{dv}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}=\frac{1}{(t-s)^{d}}\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\mathcal{H}\left(\varphi(x,\frac{x-\zeta}{t-s})+\zeta\right)\frac{dw}{\langle\frac{x-\zeta}{t-s}\rangle^{N}}\right\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}

\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{d}}\bigg[\sup_{x,\zeta}\left(\left|(\nabla_{v}\varphi)(x,\frac{x-\zeta}{t-s})\right|(t-s)^{-d}+1\right)^{-1}\bigg]\|\mathcal{H}\|_{L^{1}}\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{d}}\|\mathcal{H}\|_{L^{1}}.

Remark 8.2

Similarly, under the same assumptions of
\mathcal{H}
and
\varphi
, one has

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left|\mathcal{H}\left(\varphi(x,v)+x-(t-s)v\right)\right|\left(\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}+\frac{1}{\langle v-\frac{\alpha}{t}\rangle^{N}}\right)dv\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim\|\mathcal{H}\|_{L^{p}},

(8.3)

and for
0\leq s\leq\frac{t}{2},~t\geq 1
,

\displaystyle\left\|\int_{\mathbb{R}^{d}}\left|\mathcal{H}\left(\varphi(x,v)+x-(t-s)v\right)\right|\left(\frac{1}{\langle v\rangle^{N}}+\frac{1}{\langle v-\frac{\alpha}{t}\rangle^{N}}\right)dv\right\|_{L^{p}_{x}}\lesssim\frac{1}{t^{\frac{d(p-1)}{p}}}\|\mathcal{H}\|_{L^{1}}.

(8.4)
